tiruvallikkēṇi - 600 005.



yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷai nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmai kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunilai niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmaikaḷai ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vaittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum kaṟkum tollaiyai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tillai, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēvaiyumillai. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmaikaḷai ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉai aṭaiyalām.
- maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nākai - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭaittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulamai mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamaiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam uṭaiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟainta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivait tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittokai, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭaimaiyē eṉṉai ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ tokai curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷai varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭaiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
nuḻaivurai
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
muntai aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum takavārnta perumai peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yuraikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟai moḻikaḷukkum tantai moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻpparappāḷarāyp potunilaik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum perumai peṟṟa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭai malaiyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpatai nilai nāṭṭiya niṟaimalai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
ainteḻuttāl orupāṭai eṉṟu aṟaiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭaiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalaimali pēḻaiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟaimai pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpatai,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunilai tirital ceyyuṭ killai 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇai ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citaintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumaiyum kuṟumaiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷai ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takainiṉṟa taṇṭamiḻai - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilaikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟaimalai yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talaippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivai
 paintamiḻ kākkum paṭai
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭaiyum kalait tiṟap paṭaippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭaiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟai āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟai āṟṟalai 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalaic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattai
paṉimalai mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amaiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amaintavar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimalai - imayam; nīla āṟu - nail; amaitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷai, nūlaka iyakkat tantai araṅka nātarum, innāḷ caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakaimaippaṭutti vaittamai perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avai,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟaiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭurai 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nākai aṭikaḷai - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattaic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakaiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammaiyār! ivviṇaiyar koṭaiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉai ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷattai vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟaivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvarai payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum nilaiyil, arumait tantaiyār iyaṟkai eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉaiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyait toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmai tamiḻukku illai. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppillai eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟaikaḷaiyum iḷamaiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟaiyaiyum, nākaiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟaiyaiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟaiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟaiyāl vāṭum aṉṉaikkuc cumaiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākaiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamaiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉai māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpataiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyai niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, tokai nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvai, iṟaiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvai mukappil, ematu patiṉaintām āṇṭil muṟaippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷaiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyataiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uraiyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamaip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nākai intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc caiva cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉai maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉaipeyaril maṟuppurai eḻutiṉār. ataṉaikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷaic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉai viraivilē ceṉṉaikku varuvippōm nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉai vāḻvu amaiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇaivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉai māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumai kaṇṭu, pērāciriyaraik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uraiyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattaiyum mūtaṟivaiyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanilai ovvāmaiyāl nākaikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉai: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaraic ceṉṉaikku aḻaittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpikai eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉai kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra ak kallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟait talaivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kalaiñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkalai māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullaippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappālai (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci urai eḻutiṉār.
avvurai nūlai vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟaiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvuraip pāṅkiṉaip piṉpaṟṟip paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷai ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟai urai, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakaikaḷai iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉai viḷaittamai kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakaiya uraikaḷaiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻaintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭamoḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭattai viruppappāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉaik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikkai kuṟaiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟaiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyai muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikaimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. vaiyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikaimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭaivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉai kacaṭukaḷai nīkkiyatu? vaiyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammaic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟaic ceyta arumai eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kārikai āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārait taḻuvutal uṇmai tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapurattai vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattaiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattaiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmi kaḷ valiyuṟut tiya avviraṇṭu koḷkaikaḷaiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷai oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. innilaiyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkaikaḷaiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉaikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉaiyār ciṉṉammaiyār, tuṇaiviyār cavuntiram ammai yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvaraik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpikai, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. innilaiyilēyē 27.08.1911il caivat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭai (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷip paṭai. muṉṉēyē avarai aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvai
nām mīḷpārvai oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akavaiyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kaivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullaippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvurai vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉai; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉai; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉai - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikai yār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭamai eṉpatu, akattē amaintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷai viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyām. av veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravillai! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka nilaiyam, potunilaik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amaippu uruvākum nilaiyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avai iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa nilaiyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭait tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭait tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭait tūṇṭal valimai mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyai veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakaittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takaimai ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉai uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilaināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amaintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmai,
tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟai yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantaip pēṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉaippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟokai, arumaṇik kuvaiyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷaik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟ koṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumaimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumait tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 varai mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavai. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyavaiyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉaittai yum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vaitta kuṟuvaṭṭu kaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉamai, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷai mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. annilai yil,
ūḻaiyum uppakkam kāṇpar ulaiviṉṟit
tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyai vāymai moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷaiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amaintu tāṅkal, iyaṟkai iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟai muṉvaittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappuk kām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icaik kalaikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākarattai veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉai vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉaip pakutikaḷaiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, uraivēntar auvai cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷai vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantaiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷaiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷaiyum muḻumai muḻumaiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - tokai - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷaiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti varai veḷivanta- tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷai ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amaikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumai iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇaiyaiyum makkaḷaiyum uṟavaiyum aṉpaiyum naṇpaiyum oruṅkē iṇaittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇaippaiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
maṟaimalaiyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumai! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalaic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭait tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putaiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉaiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭai! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakaimai! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟaiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymai viḷakkamām!
puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar nilaiyam,						iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							         irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturai - 6
625006.

malaikka vaikkum maṟaimalaiyam!
patippurai
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉai vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmai uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷatai varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇarai aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattait tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭai eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭai niṉaivu kūrum vakaiyil, taṉit tamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyai eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭaippukaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimai yūṭṭiṉar.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivuk kaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻai 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭaippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavai, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavai, peyar terintum pārvaikkuk kiṭṭātavai - eṉap palavakaiyāyk kiṭaittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭaippukaḷ! avaṟṟai `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum talaippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭaivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavalaiyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt ticai nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭaipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭaiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputaiyal palar kaikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷārait tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyattai etirppatilum camaṟkirutattai vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēm pāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyait tamiḻar neñcaṅ kaḷil arumpa vaittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavaittatu taṉit tamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻaippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimaiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukaiyaiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kalaiñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitaikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvai avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta virutai civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamaippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malaimalaiyaṭikaḷ).
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyaraik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟaimiku āṅkilam maṟaivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulamai mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻaikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumaiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
 maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ maṟaiyāt tiruppeyar
 vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉaip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉai maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkaiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻaippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamillai.
ceṉṉaiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷait tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talaimuṟaikkum varuṅkālat talaimuṟaikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉai (cātaṉai)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumaiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avai kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpatai aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalattai otukki vaittu, tamiḻ nalattaik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟaivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalaip piṉṉiṇaippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻaikkum uṇarvai iḷamaiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmai eḻavaittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avarai eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamaiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟaivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavai! avaṟṟil orupakutiyai yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kaikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vai. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvattai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉaivar kaikaḷaiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumai - caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟai aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟai aṉaivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭaikkum paṇiyaic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumaiyaik karutātu uḻaikkum maṉavurattai muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷai iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭaiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
vallamai cērkkum valimaiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ naintiṭil etunammaik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻaik kākkum uṇarvaip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kaikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷai nāṭṭuṭaimaiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻaikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amaippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.

tamiḻc cāṉṟōr pēravai (1998)
tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amaitta ittaku amaippukaḷukku mūla vicaiyākat tikaḻum perumaiyaip peṟuvōr maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavai. avai toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai!
aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉaittup paṭaippukaḷum poruḷvaḻip pirikkappaṭṭu, kālavaricaiyil 34 tokuppukaḷākat tokukkap paṭṭu `maṟaimalaiyam eṉa uṅkaḷ kaikaḷil ippōtu tavaḻkiṉṟatu.
pala talaimuṟaikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum aṟivuk karuvūlam itu!
tamiḻar illantōṟum irukkavēṇṭiya aruntamiḻc celvam itu!
aṟivulakattāl `maṟaimalaiyam varavēṟkappaṭum eṉṉum nampikkai emakkuṇṭu. nūṟ curaṅkattil nuḻaiya aḻaikkiṟōm.

eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷai niṉaivuk kūṟukiṟōm.
- kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
nūlākkattiṟkut tuṇai niṉṟōr

etirmam (Negative):  pirācacu intiyā (Process India) ceṉṉai-5.
accaṭippu maṟṟum nūl kaṭṭamaippu:  
veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā						


nūlurai
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya puṉaikatai nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟu innūl. aṭikaḷār iyaṟkait tōyvum, uvamaic ceṟivum katait tiruppaṅkaḷum oṉṟai oṉṟu veṉṟu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ēṟattāḻa 300 pakkaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya innūl 1911 il kaḻaka veḷiyīṭāka vantuḷatu.
āṅkilak katai muṟai vaḻi nūlaip paṭaittiruntālum nūliṉ amaippu muḻuvatum taṉit tamiḻnāṭṭu amaippēyām eṉṟu patippurai pakarkiṟatu. itaṉaik kaṟpār taṉittamiḻ māṇpum paintamiḻp pulamaiyum, paṇṭaiya vaḻakkum, aṟivum, āṟṟalum, naṉmai vēṭṭalum, piṟarkkutavalum, āṭcit tiṟaṉum, cūḻcci vaṉmaiyum, putiya kataiyamaikkum poṟpum peṟuvār eṉpatum patippurai.
vaḻi naṭantu pōkum iḷaiñaṉ eṉat toṭaṅki (mutal atikāram) kumuta valliyum nīlalōcaṉaṉum eṉa niṟaivu ceykiṟār (patiṉāṟām atikāram).
ovvōr iṭattu vaṇṇaṉaiyum, ovvōr āḷvaṇṇaṉaiyum pakkam pakkamāka nīḷkiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. 625 ām āṇṭu iḷavēṉil kālattil eṉa varalāṟṟupputiṉap pōkkil mēl malait toṭar vaḻiyil nīlamalaic celavāy nērkiṉṟatu. cellum iḷaiñaṉ cantiraṉ eṉpāṉ. avaṉ neṭuntolaivil iruntu vantu ōr iṭukku vaḻiyil ceṉṟu kaḷvar pācaṟai oṉṟai aṭaikiṟāṉ. kaḷvar talaivaṉaiyum kāṇkiṟāṉ. 
ellām cevvaiyāka nikaḻntaṉa eṉa iḷaiñaṉ, kaḷvar talaivaṉiṭam kūṟa avaṉ, itaṉāl nākanāṭṭu araciyiṭattum nīlalōcaṉaṉ eṉṉum pautta iḷaiñaṉiṭattum karutiya vaṇṇam muṭittu vantāy eṉṟu niṉaikkiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ.
mēṟkarai eṉṉum taṉ nakaril iruntu payaṇam pōvataṟku mikavum icainta vaḻiyiṉai nīlalōcaṉaṉukkuk kāṭṭiyatu pōlavē, nākanāṭṭil iruntu varum aḻakiya kumutavallikkum kāṭṭiyirukkiṟēṉ eṉṟu av viḷaiñaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ.
kaḷvar talaivaṉ peyar nallāṉ; vanta iḷaiñaṉ nīlakiriyāṉ. kaḷvar talaivaṉ āṟu kaḷvarkaḷai aḻaittu nīlalōcaṉaṉ varum vaḻiyai nōkki yirukka viṭuttāṉ. nīlalōcaṉaṉōṭu tuṇaivar iruvar varuvatum colli viṭuttiruntāṉ. avarkaḷaic ciṟaiyākap piṭittu vara vēṇṭum eṉpatu avaṉ āṇai.
kutiraiyil muṉṉē vantavaṉ nīla lōcaṉaṉ; kuṭaku nāṭṭāṉ; maṟṟai iruvarum viyākkira vīraṉ, kēcari vīraṉ eṉpār.
ciṟai piṭikkum muyaṟci tōṟṟuppōy mūvar piṇamāka, mūvar tappi ōṭiṉar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutal mūvarum vaḻiyait toṭarntaṉar. ōriṭattil vaḻi mūṉṟākap pirintatu. aṭuttirunta kuṭicaiyil kēṭka avaṉ nīlakiri cellum vaḻiyaik kāṭṭiṉāṉ. appaṭik kāṭṭiyavaṉ kaḷvaṉ iruḷaṉē, akkuṭicai vāṇaṉ uṭaiyai vāṅki uṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉaṉām.
avvaḻi kuṟuki neṭitu ceṉṟu oru paranta veḷiyai aṭaintu niṉṟuviṭṭatu. oru kuṭicai iruntatu. paḷiṅkupōl nīrōṭai iruntatu. ataṉpāl paḷiṅkaṉṉa naṅkai orutti niṉṟāḷ. nīla lōcaṉaṉ avaḷ aḻakil mayaṅkiṉāṉ. nīlakiri cellum vaḻi taṉakkut teriyum eṉṟum tāṉum aṅkē cella iruppatākavum kūṟiṉāḷ. avaḷ tōḻiyar iruvarum uṭaṉiruntaṉar. avaḷ peyar mīṉāmpāḷ eṉṟāḷ. avaḷ taṅkum iṭam teḷinīr vēli eṉṟāḷ. nīlalōcaṉaṉum mīṉāmpāḷum muṉṉē cella maṟṟai iruvar, iruvarum piṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar. muṉṉē oru kōpuram vantatu. aṅkē taṅkiṉar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ mīṉāmpāḷuṭaṉ taṉiyaṟaiyil taṅkiṉāṉ. maṟṟaiyōr nālvarum oruṅkē ōraṟaiyil taṅkiṉar. mīṉāmpāḷ mayakka valaiyil nīlalōcaṉaṉ vīḻntu neṭunēram uṟaṅkik kēcari vīraṉāl, eḻuppappaṭṭu mayakku nīṅkiṉāṉ. nallāṉ cūḻcciyāl ciṟai vaikkappaṭa irunta nīlalōcaṉaṉ kēcari vīraṉ tiṟattāl viḻippuṟṟu nallāṉ paṭai varumuṉ tappi veḷiyēṟiṉar. avvāṟē nākanāṭṭaraci kumuta valliyum varak kūṟiya vaḻiyai māṟṟik koṇṭatāl ciṟaippaṭutta muṭiyavillai. kaḷvar talaivaṉ nallāṉ ellām tōlvi eṉa varunti vēṟu cūḻcciyil iṟaṅkiṉāṉ.
nallāṉ mātavaṉ eṉṉum ēvalaṉ uṭaiyai māṟṟik koṇṭu kumutavalli ceṉṟa vaḻi nōkki viraintu ceṉṟāṉ. oru taṅkal viṭutiyil niṉṟa kutiraikaḷaik kaṇṭu mātavaṉ kūṟiya aṭaiyāḷam teḷintu, nallāṉum ōr aṟaiyil taṅki, kumutavalli taṅkiyirukkum aṟaiyaik kaṇṭu koṇṭu ayarnta pōtil avvaṟaikkuc ceṉṟāṉ. ataṉaik kumutavalli aṟintum ayarntāḷ pōlak kāṭṭiyirukka avaḷ viralil irunta mōtirattaik kaḻaṟṟi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiṉāṉ. vēṟu aṇimaṇikaḷai eṭuttilaṉ. viḻittuk kūṟiṉāl, avaṉ katti patam pārkkum eṉpatāl kumutavalli amaintiruntāḷām. tōḻiyariṭam mōtiram iḻantālum nām nīlamalai ceṉṟu evariṭam ataṉaic cērkka vēṇṭi vantōmō, avaraik kaṇṭu uṇmaiyaic colvōm eṉa mēlum pātukāppuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭaṉar.
taṅkal viṭutiyār takka vīrarkaḷōṭu pōkac colli āḷkaḷuṭaṉ viṭuttār. avarkaḷ cellum vaḻiyaḷavum  ceṉṟu mīḷa mūvar maṭṭum toṭarntaṉar. kāṭṭuk kuṟuvaḻiyūṭē puliyoṉṟu vantu iraṇṭu vīrarkaḷai vīḻttiyatu. ammūvarum nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutalāya mūvar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ kumuta valliyiṉ parivaip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu avarkaḷukku utavuvatākak kūṟic colli uṭaṉ ceṉṟāṉ. cellum vaḻiyil mutiyar iruvar taḷarntu kaiyēnta avarkaḷukkuk kācu tantāḷ kumutavalli. uṭaṉvanta nīlalōcaṉaṉum taṉ kācu tara muyala, paiyil iruntu kācuṭaṉ kumutavalli mōtiramum vīḻakkaṇṭāḷ. ataṉai veḷikkāṭṭātavaḷāyt taṅkal viṭuti oṉṟai aṭaintāḷ. aṅkē kumutavalliyum tōḻiyarum taṅkuvataṟku ōriṭam tīrmāṉamāyiṟṟu. nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutalōr vēṟiṭattut taṅkik kālaiyil puṟappaṭat tīrmāṉittaṉar.
kumutavalli taṅkum aṟaikkup pōkum pōtē tōḻiyarai viraintaḻaittuk kutirai mēl ēṟac colli viraivuṟṟāḷ. viraintu pōṉa pōkkil ceṉṟu, taṭṭuppaṭṭa kuṭicaiyil taṅkiṉar. aṉṟiravu aṅkē taṅki mīḷavum puṟappaṭṭaṉar. nallāṉ mīṉāmpāḷaik kaṇṭu tāṉ kumutavalliyiṭam mōtiram paṟṟiya veṟṟiyai uraittu, nīlalōcaṉaic ciṟaippaṭuttat tavaṟiyatai aṟintu varunti uraiyāṭi mīḷ muyaṟci mēṟ koṇṭāṉ. mīṉāmpāḷai nīlakirikkuc cantiraṉiṭam cella ēvi, tāṉ nīlalōcaṉaṉaip paṟṟap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. tappic ceṉṟa kumutavalli cellum vaḻiyil mīṉām pāḷaik kaṇṭāḷ. iruvarum iṉitu uraiyāṭiṉar. mīṉāmpāḷ nīlakirikkut tuṇaivaruvatu pōl kāṭṭit takka iṭattuc ciṟaivaikkak karavāka eṇṇiṉāḷ.
avaḷ taṅki nekiḻvāka uraiyāṭik koṇṭirukkum pōtu karumpāmpoṉṟu maṟaintuvantu mīṉāmpāḷ kālaic cuṟṟit tīṇṭiyatu. āṅkup putitu vanta oruttiyum uṭaṉvanta tōḻi oruttiyum naccunīkkum maruttuvam pārttum nacciṉ vēlai kuṟaiyavillai, ēṟiyatu, mīṉāmpāḷ piḻaiyōm eṉṟa nilaiyil taṉ vayam irunta mōtirattait tantu nallāṉ maṉaivi tāṉeṉṟum, nallāṉukku ekkēṭum ceyyak kūṭātu eṉṟu vēṇṭiyum, āyiṉum viḻippāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyum uyir nīttāḷ.
kumutavalliyum tōḻiyarum nīlakiri cērntu, tāṅkaḷ taṅkutaṟkut tiṭṭappaṭuttiya maṉōkarar vaḷamaṉai eytiṉar. avar veḷiyūr ceṉṟiruntār eṉiṉum vēṇṭiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ aṉaittum ceytiruntār eṉpatu kēṭṭu aṅkē taṅkiṉar. maṉōkarar mīṇṭapōtu kumutavalli, avar illilē iruntu nīlalōcaṉaṉ varakkaṇṭāḷ. maṉōkarar nākanāṭṭaraci yiṭattum, nīlalōcaṉaṉiṭattum ovvoru mōtiram vaḻaṅki yiruntataiyum avaṟṟaip peṟṟu avarkaḷ kumutavalliyum nīlalōcaṉaṉumē eṉa uṟutippaṭuttik koṇṭār.
eṉ iḷaiya nēcarkaḷē nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum nākanāṭṭai orukāl aracāṇṭa kōc ceṅkaṇṇaṉ pērap piḷḷaikaḷ āvīrkaḷ eṉpatu uṇmaiyēyām eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. iruvar kaikaḷaiyum iṇaittu maṇavaṭaiyāḷam purintār. paḻaiya varalāṟṟaik kūṟi niṟaivu ceykiṟār.
- irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


kumutavalli nākanāṭṭaraci

1966il caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam patippitta nūliṉai 
mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
innūlaip paṟṟiya kuṟippurai...
putumaip pulavar putiṉap pulavarāṉār. `pūttut tōṉṟum putiṉak kalaikaḷaip pulavarkaḷ kūrntu kaṇṭu nūlkaḷaip paṭaikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttum aṭikaḷār tāmē muṉṉōṭiyāki ipputiṉak kalaiyil pukuntār.
tamiḻmaṇṇil katai vaṭippukaḷ iruntālum iḵtoru taṉikkalaiyākap piṟappeṭuttatu mēlai nāṭṭiltāṉ. `nāval eṉṉum ittāliyac colliṟkup `putumai eṉṟu poruḷ. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ittāli nāṭṭu aṟiñar tāṉiyēl tīpō eṉpār ipputumaiyai mutalil paṭaittār. ivarai ikkalaiyiṉ tantai eṉpar. ikkalaiyaiyē nām putiṉam eṉṟu tamiḻil ippōtu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
aṭikaḷār ippaṭaippai vaṭikkavēṇṭumeṉṟu muṉaintu iraṇṭu nūlkaḷaip paṭaittuḷḷār. oṉṟu `kumutavalli allatu nākanāṭṭaraci eṉṟa āṅkilap putiṉattiṉ taḻuval. ireyiṉālṭueḻutiya āṅkila nāval itu. itu tam kōṭpāṭu koḷkaikaḷaik kāṭṭi eḻuta vāyppāka iruntatai uṇarntu vaṭittār. tamiḻarmatam nīṇṭakālat tuyil nīkka ikkalaiyiṉ eḻutuvatākak kuṟittuḷḷār.
nallōr tuṉpuṟṟu iṉpuṟuvar; tuṉpam iḻaip pōr tuṉpuṟuvar; uyirppali atilum māntappali kūṭātu; kātalar kūṭuvar; kātal pūṇṭōr kaṭavuḷ nilaiyaiyum peṟuvar; nallāṭci kaṇṭōr vēṟṟuk kaṭciyai virumpār eṉṉum karuttukaḷiṉ potivu ipputiṉam. iṭaiyiṭaiyē tam kōṭpāṭukaḷākiya civam, cittāntam, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, taṉittamiḻ vaḷarttal mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum vivaramāka vaittuḷḷār. itaṉait tamiḻ marapil koṇṭucelvatai nūliṉ peyarē aṟivikkiṟatu. itaṉai ōr `aṟaneṟip putiṉam eṉalām.
- kōvai iḷañcēraṉār
tamiḻ māmalai
PREFACE

The art of fiction has, in modern times. become a potent means in the hands of educated men to awaken the finer instincts of man, so that this awakening of one’s own better part might itself serve to correct his mistakes and render him a useful and helping member of society. Within his mind exist in latency capabilities of various kinds and degrees of which one class is evoked by one particular set of outward facts and another by another set of facts. With such peculiar  occurances of outer world corresponds the manifestation of evil or good character of man. Generally speaking, the eveil part of human mind finds itself readily and easily called forth into activity by most of the surrounding objects while its better part, for want of fitting means, rarely gains access into the world outside. Now the most desirable function of a learned man should, as far as his innate abilities permit, have been to touch upon the inmost spring of man’s delicate nature and open the doorway to the region of love and virtue. But to go deep down the softest and most fertile part of human nature is not within the power of those unwary scholars who adopt improper means without discrimination. It is only those who are cautious and shrewd, those who spend apparently a greater part of their time in the selection of efficient means, in the making of it more attractive than it usually seems to be, that accomplish this very significant and well-meaning task. Take any branch of knowledge: philosophy or science, religion or literature, in their hands it becomes a very effective weapon.
Now, of all these instruments of knowledge, the fiction as an interesting branch of art appeals to all classes of people without efort and aggressiveness, and hence its place is daily becoming more and more prominent amidst them all. In almost all civilised countries but of India, especially southern India, the art of fiction has made a rapid and nearly a perfect development. But here it is still in its infancy. The host of novel writers are primarily led by mercinary and other motives. No genius has as yet arisen and no novel breathes the atmosphere of true art. In most of the Tamil novels that I have come across. I find neither plot nor character delineation, neither a true representation of life nor a chaste and felicitious diction of language. The story chiefly turns only upon love passion--the love in its most degraded type and gross earthly form. Although the Tamil language is pliant and rich in vocabulary capable of conveying the finest shades of meanings, yet in all the Tamil Novels published in a decade or two the diction is rendered very unwholesome by the introduction of unassimilated foreign words from Sanskrit and other languages and by the unhappy combination of words and phrases. In the matter of happy diction and choicest expression of thought a real and fastidious scholarship is the only necesary requirement; but of this not a single Tamil novel of recent times bears a notable mark. Even the Tamil renderings of some English Novels have been done by inelegant hands in so tasteless a manner that the beauty of the original appears in the new drees transformed into ugliness. Except a few who look upon themselves as civilised and up-to date, the main portion of the Tamils brought up in a simple and pure life do not like these novels at all. They like to read novels written in a pure and simple. Tamil style depicting the nature and human life as found in their experience but agreeably ennobled by the moral eelevation of the novelist’s ideal thought. Having keenly felt their want I ventured to produce this adapted Tamil translation of an eminent English novel in the hope that the pure and simple Tamil into which the ideas of the original are cast would please the Tamils very much leading them further and further into an appreciation of the art and study of fiction coupled with their own progress of life.
To the Tamilians of yore and their descendants the art of fiction was not an unknown thing before the introduction of modern novel. How carefully this art was studied by them would become manifest from a glance at the critical commentary of Nakkirar on Iraiyanar Agapporul written about the first century of the Christian era. And another poetic work of considerable literary merit like that of Chaucer, I mean Udayanankadai, was already in existence some six centuries before Christ, in which many a tale of Tamil antiquity appears in a collected form each of which, of course, incorporated into the main story with so profound a sense of unity that every one of them looks like to many inseparable limbs in a whole body. Besides this interweaving of one story into the other, the entire poem drawing faithful portraits from real life and tracing the distinctive features of individual character, combine them all to one complex whole so as to impress the mind of the reader with a sense of the strong unifying power of the poet. But it was a poetic age. Even story-telling went on in verse. From the domain of poetry fiction was slowly emerging to breathe the freer atmosphere of prose, when the pernicious influence of Sanscrit broke in upon the mind of the Tamil scholars and vitiated their taste for simplicity of life. A whole series of Puranic legends and obscene myths found their way into Tamil. And the Tamil people of mediaeval period having come under the influence of priests began to pay unlimited respect to mythical accounts of Gods and lost all their study of real life and with it the art of fiction also. But fortunately for us the study of English has once more opened our eyes to the real grandeur pertaining to the art of fiction; and once again has the unwholesome contact of Sanscrit begun stealthily to sap up the vitality of the Tamil language.I need, therefore, hardly say that it is obligatory on every son of the Tamil country to wake up from his long indifference and do his utmost to render his services in the cause of his own mother tongue Tamil which is, in fact, one of the few highly cultivated ancient languages of the world. I have great pleasure in saying that the following Tamil adaptation of a famous English novel was done by me to my greatest satisfaction and it is for the readers to judge how for I have succeeded in preserving the profound artistic skill of the English author in chaste, easy and elegant diction of Tamil.

THE SACRED ORDER OF LOVE, 
PALLAVARAM,	VEDACHALAM
1st of May 1911

atikāram - 1
vaḻi naṭantu pōkum iḷaiñaṉ

ulakattil mikacciṟanta malai nāṭṭaip paṟṟi varuṇittuc collap pōkiṉṟōm. teṟkēyuḷḷa kumarimuṉaiyiliruntu mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkaraip pakkamāy vaṭakku nōkkic cellukiṉṟa mēṟkaṇavāy malait toṭar eṉpatu oṉṟuṇṭu. immalait toṭaril nīlakiri malaikku arukāmaiyil uḷḷa malaināṭu iyaṟkaiyamaippiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaḷaṅkaḷ nirampi mikap polintu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. vaḷaintu vaḷaintu ōṭuṅ kāṉ yāṟukaḷālum teḷinta aruviyōṭṭaṅkaḷālum nīr ūṭṭappaṭum pūkkaḷ niṟainta veḷinilaṅkaḷum, iṉippāṉa paḻaṅkaḷ niṟainta tōppukaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭa mēṭṭu nilaṅkaḷum, kāṭcikku iṉiya pacumpul ceḻippāy vaḷarn tirukkum paḷḷattākkāṉa nilaṅkaḷum. vaḻinaṭaippayaṇam pōkiṉṟavar kaḷukkup peru makiḻcci tarukiṉṟa malaikaḷiṉ iṭaiyiluḷḷa  vaḻikaḷum vāṉil mīṉkaḷ viḷaṅkutal pōlap pallāyiram pūkkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirukkum meṉpul vaḷarntu uyarnta poṟṟaikaḷum. pañcu, caṇal, nārppaṭṭu mutaliyaṉa maṇṭi vaḷarntu kāṟṟāl alaiyuṅ kollaikaḷum āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum aṅkuḷḷa tōppukaḷiṉ uḷḷē nuḻaintu kāṇpēmāyiṉ mātuḷampaḻam, palāppaḻam, māmpaḻam, nārattampaḻam mutaliyavaṟṟaic cumaicumaiyāyt tāṅki niṟkiṉṟa maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu iruppatu kāṇalām. aṅkuḷḷa malaikaḷiṉiṭaiyiṟ cantu veḷikaḷilē uḷḷuruvip pōyk kāṇpēmāyiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷiluṅ kulai kulaiyāyp paḻaṅkaḷ toṅkukiṉṟa tīviya tirāṭcaik koṭikaḷ piṉṉal piṉṉalāyp piṇaintiruttal kāṇalām. akaṉṟa ilaikaḷai vilakkik koṇṭu puṟantōṉṟik kiṭakkuṅ kommaṭṭip paḻaṅkaḷ paḻutta koṭikaḷ tāmē eppakkat tiṉum peruviḷaiccalāyiruntalāl avviṭaṅkaḷil irukkum ēḻaik kuṭittaṉakārarum appaḻaṅkaḷai oru poruṭ paṭuttuvatillai. .ivaiyēyaṉṟip paṟaṅkippaḻam, pūcaṉip paḻaṅ kumpu kumpāyp paḻuttut toṅkum kollaikaḷaik kāṇpavar ivai iyaṟkaiyilēyē ivvāṟu koḻumaiyāy viḷain tirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyāmal kuṭikaḷāl naṉṟākat tiruttip payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟē niṉaippar. ikkollai kaḷaic cūḻntu vēli pōlirukkum putarkaḷiṉ mēṟ civakkap paḻutta koḻuṅkaṉikaḷuṭaiya kovvaik koṭikaḷum, kuṉṟimaṇik koṭikaḷum paṭarntirukkiṉṟaṉa. marap pontukaḷ tōṟum malaiveṭippukaḷ tōṟun tēṉīkkaḷ palamalar kaḷiṉiṉṟun tiraṭṭit tokutta tittippāṉa tēṉ aṭaikaḷ nirampi yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi iṅkēyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷiṉ pakkattil kāṭṭut tārākkaḷ talaiyai acaittu acaittuc celvatu kāṇalām; aravam aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa avviṭattil paṟavait tokutikaḷ paṟakkum pōtu uṇṭākum iṟakkaiyiṉ ōcaitāṉ ovvorucamayaṅ kēṭkappaṭum. nīr ōṭaikaḷil kariya aṉṉappaṟavaikaḷum veḷiya aṉṉappaṟavaikaḷum moḻu moḻuveṉṟu ōṭum nīril caṟuvalāy mitantu celkiṉṟaṉa; varakaṅ kollaikaḷil maṭappamāṉa puḷḷi māṉkaḷum kalaimāṉkaḷum mikavum viraivāyt tuḷḷit tuḷḷik kaḷippāy ōṭukiṉṟaṉa; yārum varuttuvār iṉmaiyāl muyalkaḷ kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp pulnilaṅkaḷil accamiṉṟi mēyntu tirikiṉṟaṉa; cilavēḷaikaḷil iru maruṅkum paḻutta maraṅkaḷ varicaiyāy amainta kāṭṭu neṟiyai metuvāyk kaṭantu celkiṉṟaṉa. cilavēḷaikaḷil mikavum parumaṉāṉa kaḻuku iṅkumaṅkum paṟantu pōvatu kāṇalām; āyiṉum avai arukāmaiyil uḷḷa malai uccikaḷil tām kūṭukaṭṭi vacikkiṉṟaṉa. ammalaikaḷiṉ kariya muḻaiñcukaḷiliruntu pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa aruvikaḷ uṇṭākik kīḻ aṭivārat tiluḷḷa nilattiṟ pāyntu ataṉai vaḷampaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam aṭivārattiluḷḷa vaḷappamāṉa nilamum, ataṉai aṭuttu uyarnta malaittoṭarum tammil iruvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa iyaṟkaiyuṭaiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkutalai vēṟeṅ kuṅ kāṇpatu aritu. attaṉitta malait toṭar pākaṅkaḷil malaikaḷ oṉṟaṉ mēloṉṟāy uyarntum, kuṉṟukaḷ mēṟ kuṉṟukaḷ ceṟintum, uyarattiṉ mēl uyaramāyk kaṟpāṟai kaḷum, accam vāynta ceṅkuttāṉa koṭikaḷum iruṇṭa vaḻi kaḷum, aruvi viḻuntu kuṭainta perum paḷḷaṅkaḷum, paṉip pāṟaikaḷum niṟaintum oruṅkupaṭṭut tōṉṟum mika uṉṉatat tōṟṟamāṉatu mika uyarcciyuṭaiyatāyum, kaṇṭārkku uḷḷa mēmpāṭṭai viḷaivikkum peruntakaimai yuṭaiyatāyum irukkiṉṟatu. ā! iṅṅaṉam nām varuṇittuk kūṟiya aṭivārat tiṉaiyum viyakkaṟpālatām avvuṉṉata malait toṭari ṉaiyum mēṟkuvintuḷḷa vāṉam mēkamiṉṟi nīlaniṟañ ciṟantu teḷivuṭaiyatāy virintu evvaḷavu paricuttamāy viḷaṅku kiṉṟatu!
koccimutalāṉa teṉṉāṭukaḷiliruntu mēṟpakkamāy vaṭatēcañ celluvōr tam iṭakkaippuṟattil virintu kiṭakkum im malaiyanāṭu iṅṅaṉam pēraḻakuṭaiyatāyc ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭu koḷka. nīlakiri malaiccāraliluḷḷa immalaināṭu cēra vamicattārkku urimaiyuṭaiyatāyiṉum akkālattil aracāṇṭa naraciṅkavaruma cōḻaṉ eṉṉum vēntarvēntaṉ cāḷukkiya nāṭṭu aracar mutalāyiṉāraip puṟaṅkaṇṭu pērāṟṟaluṭaiyaṉāy viḷaṅkiṉamaiyāl, maṟṟait tamiḻnāṭṭu vēntarāṉa cēra pāṇṭiyar ivaṉōṭu etirkka añci amaintiruppa malaināṭṭuk kōṭiyiluḷḷa nīlakiri malaiccāral ataṉai yaṭuttuḷḷa cōḻa nāṭṭōṭu oruṅku cērntu cōḻa vamicattārkku uriyatu pōl tōṉṟiṟṟu. naraciṅkavaruma cōḻaṉum annāṭu taṉakku urimai yuṭaiya taṉṟātal naṉkuuṇarntu ataṉai muṟṟun taṉvayappaṭutta niṉaiyāmal taṉpaṭaiyil oru pākattai aṅku nilaippittu aṅku naṭattappaṭum vāṇikam cevvaiyāka naṭaipeṟum poruṭṭu ataṉaik kāppatu pōl avinayittuvantāṉ. annīlakiri nāṭṭil uḷḷōrum vēṟṟaracaṉ oruvaṉukku nām aṭaṅki oḻukak kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷōm eṉpatai niṉai yāmal, tām virumpiyavāṟu tammuṭaiya ciṟṟaracaṉ kīḻ iṉitu kālaṅkaḻittu vantārkaḷ. im malaiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa makkaḷ aḻakāy iruppar, āṇmakkaḷ ellārum nīṇṭuyarntu oṭintu viḻuvatu pōṉṟa aḻakiya uṭampum pārvaikku nalla tōṟṟamum uṭaiyarāy iruppar; peṇmakkaḷ ellārum tam uṭampiṉ teḷivāṉa cāyalāṉum, uṟuppukkaḷiṉ mikat tiruntiya amaippāṉum, avvuṟuppukkaḷellām orē oḻuṅkāyk kaṉinta aḻakuṭaimaiyāṉum, ulakameṅkum piracitti peṟṟuc ciṟakkiṉṟārkaḷ.
ki.pi, 625-vatu varuṭam iḷavēṉiṟ kālattiṭaiyil orunāḷ or iḷaiñaṉ mikavum aḻakāṉa oru kutirai mēl ēṟik koṇṭu nām muṉṉē varuṇittuc colliya malaiya nāṭṭil vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉukkup patiṉeṭṭu vayatu irukkalām. ivaṉ pārvaikku nalla tōṟṟam uṭaiyaṉāyum iruntāṉ ivaṉ viḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟa oruvakaiyāṉa cuṟucuṟuppu, ivaṉ uruvameṅkum kaḷḷat taṉmaiyuṭaiyatōr tōṟṟattai viḷai vittut tiṭukkiṭac ceytalāl ivaṉai aḻakuṭaiyavaṉ eṉṟu collakkūṭa villai. maṟṟu, ivaṉ uṟuppukaḷ mikac cevvaiyāṉa amaippuṭaiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa. cilavēḷaikaḷil ivaṉ kaṇṇiṉ tōṟṟattai nōkkukiṉṟavarkku ivaṉ vairam, vañcaṅ, koṭumai mutalāṉa tīya kuṇaṅkaḷu ṭaiyavaṉ pōl kaṭumaiyāyt tōṉṟum. āyiṉum, malai nāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟa varkku ittakaiya pārvai potuvāka iruttalāl, itu koṇṭē avar kuṇaṅkaḷai aḷantaṟital oru ciṟitum poruntātu. iṅṅaṉam nām varuṇikkiṉṟa ivviḷaiñaṉuṭaiya uṭampiṉ cāyal peṇkaḷukku iruppatu pōl teḷivāyt tikaḻntatu; āyiṉum makaḷirkku uḷḷa meṉmaiyillai; avaṉ kaṉṉaṅkaḷil kaṭaṟcippiyiṟpōṟ civanta vaṇṇat tōyccal illaiyāyiṉum iḷam paruvattiṟku uriya civanta niṟañ ceḻumaiyāy iruntatu, mōvāyil mayir illai, mīcaimayir talaimayirai viṭa mikak kaṟukaṟuttu arumpiyiruntatu, talai mayir paḻuppu niṟamāyp paṭṭup pōl vaḻuvaḻuppāy iruntatu; immayiraip piṭarivaraiyil naṟukkit toṅka viṭṭut talaiyiṟ poṟcarikai viḷimpukōtta ciṟupākai cūṭiyirun tāṉ, uṭampiṉōṭu oṭṭi iṟukap piṭittirukkum kāṟ caṭṭai, kaiccaṭṭai yiṭṭiruppataṉāl pūṅkompu pōl oṭintuviḻum iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya avaṉ uṭampiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷ tiruttamāṉa aḻakuṭaṉ poruttamuṟṟiruttal naṉku tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. avaṉ iṭuppil iṟukap piṇittirukkum araikkaccil ilēcāṉa koṭuvāḷ oṉṟu toṅkiṉatu, melliya tōliṉāl taitta aṭiccaṭṭai muḻaṅkāl aḷavum māṭṭi yiruntāṉ. avaṉ ēṟic celluṅ kutiraiyiṉ cēṇaṅ kaliṉam mutaliyaṉa ellām naṭuttaramāṉa ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa; curuṅkac collu miṭattu, ivviḷaiñaṉ periya pirapu vīṭṭiluḷḷa ēvalāḷi kaḷil uyarnta nilaiyil uḷḷavaṉākat tōṉ ṟiṉāṉ.
iṉi oruvakaiyāṉa kuṟippuḷḷa ivaṉ kaṇkaḷ peruttu nīla niṟamuṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟippai nōkkāmal avai teḷivāka iruttalai māttiram pārppavarkku avai mikavum aḻakākat tōṉṟum, nalla  kuṇaṅkaḷukku ōr uṟai viṭam pōlat tōṉṟi akaṉṟu uyarnta neṟṟi poṉṉiṟamāy maḻamaḻaveṉṟu viḷaṅkutalāl avaṉ kaṇkaḷil uḷḷa koṭuṅkuṟippu avvaḷavākat terita lillai. avaṉ mukattiṉ kīḻppākaṅkaḷellām avaṉ nallaṉ eṉṟaṟiyumpaṭiyāka amaintiruntaṉa. mikac ceḻumai yāka avaṉ itaḻkaḷ avaṉiṭattuk kaḷḷaṅkavaṭu illāmai kāṭṭuvaṉa pōl iruntaṉa. avvitaḻkaḷ viriyuntōṟum iṭaiyē mayakkun taṉmaiyuṭaiya nakaiyoḷi tōṉṟutal pōla maṟṟai āṇ makkaḷiṭattil pārttalaritu. aṅṅaṉam nakaitōṉṟum pōtellām, neyppāṉa pavaḷat tuṇṭiṉmēl ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir virital pōlavum, iṅkulikac ceppiṉ vaṭimpil viḻumiya muttukkaḷ patittatupōlavum iraṇṭu varicaiyākap paṟkaḷ tōṉṟum. 
iṉi ivvāṟu varuṇittuc collappaṭṭa iḷaiñaṉ kuṭakilirun tāyiṉum, iṉṉum ataṟkun tolaiviluḷḷa nākanāṭṭiliruntāyiṉum vantavaṉpōlmēl kaṭaṟkaraiyaic cārnta mēṟkaṇavāy malaiyanāṭṭil vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉ nīlakiri nakarattiṟku nērē pōkum vaḻiyiṟ cellāmal iṭaiyē mēṟkaṇvāy malaiccāraliṟ koṇṭupōyviṭuṅ kuṟukalāṉa oru cantu vaḻiyil ippōtu tirumpic ceṉṟāṉ. ivaṉ cila nāṭkaḷāka vaḻinaṭantu vantirukkavēṇṭum; ēṉeṉil, ciṟitu tūramāttiram payaṇam paṇṇiyiruntāl taṉkutiraiyaip paṟṟi ippōtu kavalai koṇṭu pārppatu pōl pārkkavēṇṭuva tillaiyām; ippōtu ataṉaic ciṟitu nērattiṟku oru taram niṟuttik kīḻ iṟaṅki nilattiṟ koḻumaiyāy vaḷarntirukkum iṉiyapul mēyaviṭṭut tāṉ tīvitirāṭcak koṭi paṭarnta maraniḻalil iruntu cāyntu koṇṭu talaikkumēlē kuṭumpukuṭumpāyt toṅkumpaḻak kulaiyil oṉṟupaṟittut tiṉṉuvāṉ; tirumpavuñ ciṟitu nēraṅkaḻittup payaṇam paṇṇuvāṉ; ivvāṟu cila nāḻikaiyāka avaṉ vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ.
iṉi ivaṉpōkuñ cantuvaḻi mika nīṇṭatā yiruntālum metteṉṟa puṟkaṟṟai neṭuka viruttalāṟ kutiraikku vaḻiyiṟ ciṟitum varuntamillai. atuvēyumiṉṟi avvaḻiyi liṭai yiṭaiyē pala ciṟṟaruvikaḷ ōṭivaruvatāl kutirai taṉ kālkaḷaik kuḷirac ceytukoṇṭu taṇṇīr aruntit tākattaiyun taṇittuk koḷvatāyiṟṟu. iṉi ñāyiṟu maṟaikiṉṟa camayattil ivviḷaiñaṉ accantuvaḻiyiṉ muṭiviṟpōyc cērntāṉ; naṭuvil iṭaiveḷi yāyuḷḷa mēṭṭuppāṅkāṉa nilattiṟku irupakkattum ceṅkuttāy āḻntukiṭakkum paḷḷattākkuḷḷa malaiccāral toṭar puṟṟu irukkiṉṟatu. im mēṭṭuppāṅkāṉa malait toṭarpi ṉuḷ avaṉ nuḻaintu pōvāṉāyiṉāṉ; iṅṅaṉam pōkap pōka iruḷōveṉṟu karukittōṉṟum malait tōṟṟam avaṉ cilanāḻikai muṉṉē vaḻinaṭantuvanta vaḷaviya nilattiṉ ṟaṉmaiyōṭu evvaḷavu māṟupaṭṭuk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu! mālaippoḻutu muṟṟum vantu iruṇṭavuṭaṉē, karaṭu muruṭāṉa immalaippāṅkil virintu kiṭakkum oru kāṭṭukku arukēvantu cērntāṉ. avaṉ aṅkuḷḷa vaḻittuṟaika ḷellām muṟṟun terintavaṉ pōlavum, aṅku etaṟkum añcātavaṉ pōlavuṅ kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. maraṅkaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu piṇaintu vaḻituṟaiyiṉṟi ellām kuḻampalāyk kiṭakkum avviruṇṭakāṭṭiṉ iṭaiyē putitāka vaḻiccelvōr yārkkum aṟiyakkūṭāta vaḻiyai ivaṉ kūrmaiyāṉa kaṇkaḷ terinteṭuttaṉa; piṉṉum ōr arai nāḻikaiyil taḻaik kumpiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē veḷiccan tōṉṟutaluṅ kaṇṭukoṇṭāṉ; maṟṟuñ cila nimiṣaṅ kaḷuḷ, maraṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil kuṟukku uttiraṅ kaṭṭi ataṉmēl veḷḷāṭṭut tōlāṉum māṉṟōlāṉun taitta pōrvai yiṭṭuc camaitta āṟu kūṭāraṅkaḷuḷḷa oru pācaṟaiyiṉai aṭaintaṉaṉ.
iṉi ippācaṟai vīṭṭu muṟṟattiṉ iraṇṭiṭattil neruppuk koḻuntuviṭṭu erintatu; in neruppaṇṭaiyil cilar mālaik kālattiṟku vēṇṭum uṇavu camaittukkoṇ ṭiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ellārum nīṇṭuyarntu valiyarāyum aḻakāyum iruntaṉar. talaiyil civappukkuñcan toṅkum āṭṭuttōṟkullā aṇintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṭiyi-liṭṭirukkum tōṟcaṭṭai kālukkup pātukāvalā yiruppa taṉṟi naṭaiviraivukkut taṭai ceyvatillai. atu vallāmal, kaittup pākkiyum ciṟṟuṭaivāḷuñ cerukiya araip paṭṭi kaiyil kūriyakattiyum toṅkaviṭṭiruntārkaḷ. kūṭāraṅ kaḷuḷ eṭṭippārttāl evvaḷavu āṭkaḷ iruntārkaḷō avvaḷavu tuppākkikaḷum iruntaṉa. curuṅkac colluṅkāl, immalaiyanāṭṭil neṭuṅkālam vaḻippaṟi koḷḷai ceytukoṇṭu varupavarāyum, innāṭṭiṟ pukunta naraciṅka varuma cōḻaṉpaṭaikkuk koṭumpakaivarāyumuḷḷa kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṉaruḷ oru pakutiyiṉarāka iṅkuḷḷa ic ciṟukūṭṭat tār kāṇappaṭṭaṉar.
iṉi ippācaṟai vīṭṭaṇṭai vantukoṇṭirukkum iḷaiñaṉ taṉkutiraik kuḷampaṭiyiṉ ōcai acciṟu kūṭṭattār kātiṟ paṭumeṉpatu terintavuṭaṉē avaṉ taṉ itaḻkaḷaik kuvittup paḷīreṉṟu oru cīḻkai yaṭittāṉ. accīḻkkai ōcai akkāṭeṅkum uruvic ceṉṟatu. akkaḷvar kūṭṭattār taṅkaḷ naṇpaṉ varavai aṟivataṟku iccīḻkkai yōcai ōr aṭaiyāḷam pōlum! mikavum tiṇintu piṉṉikkiṭattalpaṟṟi annāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē naṭattikkoṇṭu vanta taṉ kutiraiyōṭu avviḷaiñaṉ avar etiriṟ pukumpoḻutu neruppiṉ veḷiccam avaṉ mukattiṟpaṭavē avarellārum uṭaṉē avaṉait terintukoṇṭaṉar. avaṉō avarkaḷaiyum avviṭattaiyum putitukaṇṭavaṉāy illai; avarkaḷum avvāṟē avaṉōṭu naṉku paḻakiṉavar kaḷaippōl iruntaṉar. naṇparkkuriya mukamaṉuraikaḷ oruvarkkoruvar vaḻaṅkiṉar; avarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ av viḷaiñaṉuṭaṉ vanta kutiraiyaik koṇṭupōyt tīṉi mutaliyaṉa aḷittāṉ.
uṇmaiyākavē itu nērttiyāṉa kutirai eṉṟu oruvaṉ ataṉ kaḻuttiṟ ṟaṭṭikkoṭuttu uṉakku itu nalla utavi ceytatu eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ.
ām eṉṟu avviḷaiñaṉ maṟumoḻi tantu eṉakku iṉṉum neṭunēram iruntatu paṟṟi innāḷ muḻumaiyum mikametuvāy ataṉai naṭattikkoṇṭuvantēṉ. uṅkaḷ talaivaṉait tavaṟāmaṟkāṇuñcamayam naṉku aṟivēṉātalāl irāppoḻutiṟkumuṉ iṅkuvara muyaṉṟilēṉ eṉac coṉṉāṉ.
vēlaiyiruntāl oḻiya eṉṟu akkaḷvaṉ aruki luḷḷa tī veḷiccattil oru kuṟipput tōṉṟanakaittu eṅkaḷ talaivaṉukku ellānēramum oṉṟutāṉ. atu niṟka, uṉ kutiraiyaippaṟṟip pēcumiṭattu eṉṟu toṭarntu kutirai māmicattiṟ paḻakkappaṭṭavarpōl ataṉ uṭampiṉ oḻuṅkai yum ovvōr uṟuppiṉ amaivaiyum metuvāka aḷantu nōkki ā itu mika uyarnta pirāṇi eṉa moḻintāṉ.
intac cātikku uriya aṭaiyāḷam iṅkē iruk kiṉṟatu. eṉṟu avviḷaiñaṉ ataṉ piṉ ṟoṭaiyil tīykkappaṭṭa oru ciṟiya taḻumpaik kuṟittuk kāṭṭi avaṉōṭu ottup pēciṉāṉ; ēṉeṉil, ammalaināṭṭiluḷḷa kutiraikaḷiṉ cātip pakuppiṉai vaḻuvātu neṟippaṭap pōṟṟi vaippataṟku avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉam aṭaiyāḷam iṭutal vaḻakkam.
uṇmai! intac cāti nallatu tāṉ eṉṟu ataṉaip pārttuk koṇṭirunta maṟṟoruvaṉ viyantāṉ. āyiṉum, eṅkaḷ talaivaṉ ēṟic celuttukiṉṟa kutiraiyiṉum itu tāḻntatutāṉ. ēṉeṉṟāl, ataṉpiṉ toṭaiyil ilāṭak kuṟi yiruppataṉai nāṉ collumpōtu iḷaiyōy, nīyē aṟivāy eṉṟāṉ.
naṇpaṉē, niṉ talaivaṉ kutiraiyiṉai nāṉ aṟiyēṉ pōlavum, kutiraiccātikaḷuḷ atumika arumai yāṉa teṉpataik kuṟikkum ilāṭa aṭaiyāḷattai nāṉ kāṇēṉ pōlavum nī pēcukiṉṟāy. atu niṟkaṭṭum, eṉakkāka eṉ kutiraiyiṉaip pārttukkoḷ; uṅkaḷ talaivaṉōṭu nāṉ uṭaṉē pēcalvēṇṭum eṉṟu avviḷaiñaṉ moḻintāṉ.
iraṇṭāvatu pēciṉa kaḷvaṉ appaṭiyē avaṉait taṉpiṉṉē varumpaṭi aḻaittuk koṇṭu pōvāṉā yiṉāṉ. vaḻituṟaiyillāta akkāṭṭiṉ iṭaiyilē iruvarum pukuntu kūṭāra muṟṟattil erintu koṇṭirunta neruppu veḷiccat tiṟku appuṟam pōy viṭṭārkaḷ; eṅkum orē iruḷāy iruntatu. āṉāṟ kaḷvaṉō tāṉ cellum vaḻiyiṉai mikavum naṉṟāy aṟintavaṉpōl viraivāka nērē ceṉṟāṉ. iḷaiñaṉō avaṉpiṉṉē oṭṭik koṇṭu pōyiṉāṉ. ōcaiyiṉṟi eṅkum iruṇṭukiṭakkum avviṭattil caṭutiyilē yārō kāvalāḷaṉ aṟaikūvum ōr oli kēṭṭatu. uṭaṉē ataṟkuk, kūṭappōkuṅ kaḷvaṉ etō viṭai coṉṉāṉ, ciṟitu tūrattil maraṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil maṟṟoru veḷiccan tōṉṟac cila nimiṣaṅkaḷil oru kūṭārattiṟku muṉṉē tī eriyum muṟṟattil iruvarum pōyc cērntārkaḷ.
muṉṉēkaṇṭa  pācaṟai vīṭṭaik kāṭṭiṉum ik kūṭāram periyatāyum uyarnta taṉmaiyuṭaiyatāyum iruntatu; itaṉpaṭāttiluḷḷa tōliṉ ōraṅkaḷil nīlappaṭṭut taikkappaṭṭiruntatu; itaṉ nuḻaivāyilum, nīlappaṭṭiṉāṟ ceḻumaiyāṉa pūttoḻil ceyyappaṭṭa tirai iṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. tīyil maṇaṅkamaḻa uṇavu camaikkum maṭpāṇṭat taip pārttuk koṇṭē ōr ēvaṟkāraṉ uṭkārntiruntāṉ. iḷaiñaṉai aḻaittuk koṇṭu vanta kaḷvaṉ kūṭārat tuḷḷē avaṉai nuḻaintu pōkumpaṭi colli viṭṭut tāṉ veḷiyē niṉṟuviṭṭāṉ.
akkūṭārattiṉuḷḷē nilattil virikkappaṭṭa pāyiṉ mēl irupattumūṉṟu allatu irupattunāṉku vayatiṟku mēṟpaṭāta oruvaṉ cāyntu koṇṭiruntāṉ; avaṉ mukattil piṟarai aṭakkiyāḷum poruṭṭu iyaṟkaiyilēyē amaintuḷḷa orukaḷai tōṉṟiṟṟu. avaṉ uyaramākavum mellitākavum iruntāṉāyiṉum. ottuc camainta avaṉ uṭampum, cevvaiyākap poruttappaṭṭa uṟuppukaḷum avaṉ mey valiyiṉ mikutiyiṉai iṉitu viḷakkiṉa. avaṉ talaimayir kariyatāy mika aṭarntiruntatu. avvāṟē mikavum kariyavāṉa avaṉ kaṇkaḷ perum pirakācamuṭaiyaṉavā yiruntaṉa. avaṉ mukattiluḷḷa maṟṟai uṟuppuk kaḷellām kaṭaitteṭuttaṉa pōl iṉitamain tiruppavāyiṉa; avaṉ mukattai oru pakkac cāyvāyp pārttāl māttiram mūkku kaḻukupōṟ ciṟitu vaḷaivāyiruttal tōṉṟum. kaṉṉa mīcaiyum mīcaiyuṅ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mōvāy mayiriṉṟi naṉṟāyc ciraikkap paṭṭiruntatu. kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṟku uriya vaṇṇam avaṉ aṇintirunta āṭai taṉ āṭkaḷ aṇintiruntavaṟṟaik kāṭṭilum uyarntatāyum. araik kaccup pūttoḻil ceyyappaṭṭatāyum kaittulakkum, curikaiyum veḷḷippiṭi yuṭaiyaṉavāyum, kuṟṟuṭaivāḷum avvāṟē maṇikaḷ aḻuttiya piṭiyuḷḷatāyum. pāyiṉmēl avaṉ arukiṟ kiṭanta cuḻal tuppākki tiṟamaiyāṉa toḻiṟpāṭu uṭaiyatāyum viḷaṅkiṉa. kūṭārattiṟ ṟoṅkaviṭṭirunta veḷḷi viḷakku ivaṉ mukattil oḷiparappiṉamaiyāl ivaṉ mukakkuṟippai naṉku aḷantu pārttu ivaṉ māṭṭu yārkkum añcāta oru vaṉkaṇmaiyum, maṉa ūkkamum, evaṟṟaiyum poruḷ ceyyātu muṭikkum pērāṟṟalum peruntiṟamaiyum amaintu kiṭattalai eḷitilē aṟintukoḷḷalām. avaṉ maṟṟaiyōraik kāṭṭilun taṉakkuḷḷa uyarcciyiṉaiyum naṉṟāy aṟintirun tāṉ. iṅṅaṉam avaṉ ellāvakaiyāṉun taṉ kīḻ uḷḷār. taṉcoṟpaṭi aṭaṅki naṭantu taṉ iṭattu aṉpuṭaiyavarāy naṭantu koḷḷavum, tāṉvēṇṭum poḻutu avaraivalintu oṉṟu ceyyumpaṭi vaṟpuṟutti muṭikkakkūṭiya āṟṟalum uyarcciyum uḷḷavaṉāyiruntaṉaṉ.
avviḷaiñaṉukku ikkaḷvar talaivaṉ putiyaṉ allaṉ. kūṭārattiṉuḷ nuḻaintavuṭaṉē avviḷaiñaṉ ivaṉai mikavum mariyātaiyuṭaṉ vaṇaṅka attalaivaṉuñ cilacamayaṅkaḷil uyarntōr tammiṉum tāḻntōrai naṇpuṭaṉ aṉpāy naṭattu muṟaippaṭi nēyarkkuriya iṉiya moḻikaḷ colli aḻaittāṉ.
cantirā, niṉvarukai naṉṟākuka. eṉṉaceyti koṇṭu vantāy? eṉṟu akkaḷvar talaivaṉ kēṭpāṉā yiṉaṉ.
ataṟku ellāñ cevvaiyāka nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avviḷaiñaṉ maṟumoḻi tantiṭṭāṉ.
.itaṉāl, nākanāṭṭaraci yiṭattum nīlalōcaṉaṉ eṉṉum pautta iḷaiñaṉiṭattuṅ karutiya vaṇṇam kāriyattai muṭittu vantāyeṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ attalaivaṉ.
mēṟkarai eṉṉun taṉ nakarattiliruntu payaṇam pōvataṟku mikavum icaintavaḻiyiṉai nīlalōcaṉaṉukkuk kāṭṭiyatu pōlavē, nākanāṭṭiliruntu varum kumuta vallikkuṅ kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu av viḷaiñaṉ ottukkūṟiṉāṉ.
cantirā, naṉku ceytāy eṉṟu taṉmukattil makiḻcci tōṉṟac coṉṉa attalaivaṉ mīṇṭum muṉṉorumuṟai nī eṉakku mikavum nuṭpamāy eṭuttuccoṉṉa varalāṟṟil uṉakku nampikkai uṇṭō eṉpataṉait tirumpavum aṟiya virumpukiṉṟēṉ. ēṉeṉil peṟappaṭukiṉṟa apporuḷiṉ uyarcciyai nōkkiṉallatu, avvaḷavu periyatōr apāyakaramāṉa kāriyattiṟpukuvataṟku nāṉ niṉaikka māṭṭēṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ.
nāṉ nuṇukkamāy virittuccolliya ovvoṉṟum uṇmaiyāmeṉṟē nampukiṉṟēṉ. tāmaraivēli eṉṟu collutaṟkup peritum vāyppuṭaiya malaiyavēliyiṉkaṇ apperum poruḷ putaiyalāy irukkiṉṟatu eṉṟāṉ cantiraṉ.
putumai! putumai! immēṟkaṇavāy malaittoṭar muḻuvatum eṉakkut teriyāta cantu, pontu, mūlai,muṭukku oṉṟum illaiyeṉṟu eṉṉaip peritāka eṇṇiyirunta eṉakkuntaṭṭuppaṭāmal tāmaraivēli eṉpatoṉṟu iruttal peritum viyappāyirukkiṉṟatu! aṅṅaṉam oṉṟirukkumeṉa ituvaraiyil nāṉ aiyappaṭṭatu millaiyē! āyiṉum, appaṭiyoṉṟu irukkalām:- appaṭiyoṉṟu irukkattāṉ vēṇṭum eṉṟu kātiṟkēṭkum paṭiyākavē attalaivaṉ muṉakiṉāṉ.
aḵtappaṭittāṉ! uyarntōy. malai nāṭarkkuḷ vaḻaṅkum ic ceytiyāvatu umakku eṭṭiyirukkavēṇṭumē eṉṟu cantiraṉ aḻuttip pēciṉāṉ.
ām! ataṉālētāṉ nī mutaṉmutal akkataiyiṉai eṉakkuc coṉṉapōtu nāṉ avvaḷavu kavaṉamāyk kēṭṭēṉ. nāṉ kuḻantaiyāyiruntapōtu eṉ peṟṟōr - eṉṟu collum pōtē attalaivaṉ mukamvēṟupaṭap perumūccuviṭṭu ām nāṉ kuḻantaiyāyiruntapōtu iṟantupōṉa eṉpeṟṟōr, tamiḻarkku mutaṟṟantaiyuntāyum iṟaivaṉāṟ paṭaikkappaṭṭa kālattil immalaittoṭar iṭaiyil uḷḷa tāmaraivēliyil avviṟaivaṉ kaṭṭaḷai yāl taṅkiyiruntaṉareṉavum, avarukkuppiṉ makkaḷ yārum ataṟkuṭ pukuta iṭam peṟuvatillai eṉavum, āyiṉum orō vorukāl neṭuṅkālam iṭaiyiṭṭut tiruvaruḷāṇaiyāṟ celuttappaṭukiṉṟa paricuttaṉāṉatuṟavi paricuttamāṉa av viṭattaik kuṟuki āṇṭuc cuṟṟilum uyarntu viḷaṅkum malaikaḷiṉ naṭuviṟ kīḻē polintutōṉṟum iṉpamāṉa iḷaṅkāviṉai kāṇpaṉ eṉavum colvatu vaḻakkam eṉṟāṉ.
eṉṉuṭaiya kataiyāṉum nīṅkaḷ colliyavaṟṟāṉuṅ kuṟippikkappaṭṭa avviṭantāṉ nantamiḻarkku mutaṟṟantaiyun tāyum uṟainta iṉpanilameṉṟu nāṉ collavillai. eṉ aṟivukkup pulappaṭṭapaṭi kēṭṭāl, aḵtu avviṭam aṉṟeṉpatē eṉ karuttu. nammutaṟṟantai tāyārkkuppiṉ avviṭam aḻintu pōyiṟṟeṉal tāṉ uṇmai; āyiṉum immēṟkaṇavāy malaittoṭaril eṅkō ōriṭattil avar irunta ammalaiyavēlip peyarkoṇṭa ōriṉpa iḷaṅkā viḷaṅkutal vēṇṭumeṉpatu māttiram nāṉ uṟutiyākac colvēṉ. iṉi nāṉ coṉṉa maṟṟai varalāṟukaḷaik koṇṭu, aṅkup poṟkuviyal uṇṭō illaiyō eṉpataṉai nīṅkaḷē aṟintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, nāṉ coṉṉatu malaiyanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum varalāṟṟuṭaṉ mikavum poruntiyirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu cantiraṉ coṉṉāṉ.
avvaralāṟṟiṉāl, atu pōṉṟa iṉpamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ immalaittoṭariṟ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avviṭaṅ kaḷil mikac ciṟanta malarkaḷ māttiram pūkkiṉṟaṉa, arumaiyāṉa iḷamaraṅkaḷ māttiraṅkoḻukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, pāmpu mutalāṉa ūrvaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṇavākuntaḻaikaḷ aṅkillai eṉṟum, amiḻtamayamāyuḷḷa at taṉitta iṭaṅkaḷil, pulika ḷēṉum narikaḷēṉum nuḻaiya māṭṭā eṉṟum, avviṭattaic cuṟṟi araippaṭṭikaipōl uḷḷa ceṅkuttāṉa malaikaḷ puṟattēyiruntu pārppavarkkut tirai pōl avar pārvaiyiṉait taṭaiceytalē yaṉṟi veḷiyili runtu pōm makkaḷ aṭiccuvaṭu tōyāvaṇṇam eḻuppiya varampupōlavum kuḷirkālattuk koṭuṅkuḷir kāṟṟum vēṉiṟkālattu veṅkatirveppamum nuḻaiyāvāṟu kaṭṭiya araṇpōlavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, kaṭaiciyākap poṟkuviyalum veḷḷikkuviyalum putaiyalāyuḷḷa kaṉikaḷum uyarnta muḻumaṇikaḷ kuvinta muḻaiñcukaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum nām viḷaṅka aṟikiṉṟōm. eṉṟu perukiyeḻunta makiḻcci taṇintāṟpōlat tāḻnta kuraliṟ pēciṉāṉ attalaivaṉ.
paramparai paramparaiyākac collappaṭṭu immalai nāṭarkkuḷ vaḻaṅkivarum avvaralāṟṟiṉai nīr num uḷḷattiṟpotintu vait tiruntum, nāṉcoṉṉa akkatai yiṉai nīr oru kaṇamāvatu aiyamuṟattalaippaṭṭatu eṉakku mikavum viyappāyirukkiṉṟatu eṉac cantiraṉ coṉṉāṉ.
nāṉ ataṉai aiyappaṭavillai; mutaliliruntē nāṉ ataip paṟṟi aiyappaṭāviṭṭālum, nī coṉṉavaṟṟai ellām uṇmaiyākavē nampic coṉṉāyō eṉṟaṟi yattāṉ palakēḷvikaḷ kēṭṭēṉ eṉṟu etaṟkum añcāta taṉ iyalpukku icaiyaip poṟumaiyāṉa kuralōṭu attalaivaṉ pēciṉāṉ.
nāṉ coṉṉavaṟṟil nīr ēṉtāṉ aiyappaṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu cantiraṉ kēṭṭāṉ.
avaṉ eṉ nilaimaiyil uḷḷa oruvaṉ taṉ āḷ colvatai aiyappaṭāviṭṭālum oru tarattiṟku iraṇṭu taram ōr uṇmaiyai āyntaṟiyavēṇṭāmā? eṉṟu viraintu kēṭṭuc cantirā! nāṉ colvataikkēḷ. uṉṉaik kaṭaiciyāka nāṉ kaṇṭatumutal nāṉ niṉaittatu itu! nī coṉṉa kalavaramāṉa upāyattil talaikīḻāy viḻuntu varuntu talaikāṭṭiṉum, avaṉ mēṟpāyntu viḻuntu avvirakaciyam terinta avaṉaikkoṇṭē ataṉaitterintu koḷḷal nallataṉṟō! eṉṉai nampu; uṉ talaivaṉai orumuṟai iṅkupiṭittukkoṇṭu vantu vaittukkoḷvēṉāyiṉ - eṉpataṟkuḷ;
avaṉ cettāluñ cāvāṉ oḻiya ivvirakaciyattai māttirañ collāṉ eṉṟu cantiraṉ iṭaiyiṟkūṟi ik kāriyaṅkaḷellām eṉakkutterintapiṉ, avaṉ kaṭaiciyākap payaṇappaṭṭup pōṉapōtu avaṉ kūṭavē pōvataṟku nāṉ camayam pārttuk koṇṭirukkavillaiyā- eṉṟu col vataṟkuḷ;
nallatu nallatu eṉṟu attalaivaṉ avacaramāy iṭaimaṭakkic cantirā, nī coṉṉavāṟē ākaṭṭum! ataik kuṟittu nāṉ ārāyumpōtu, nī ceyta upāyappaṭi ceytalaiviṭa vēṟuvaḻiyillai, avvupāyam ciṟantateṉṟu uraippataṟkum nāṉ piṉvāṅkēṉ. periyanaṉmai kiṭaippa tāyiṉ, atu mikavuṅkalavaramāṉa upāyameṉṟu coṉṉa     
taṉāl uṇṭākum iṭaiñcalkaḷai niṉaintu piṉ vāṅkuvēṉ eṉṟu niṉaiyātē, naṇpā cantiraṉē, ceytu muṭikkakkūṭiya kāriyattiṟku eḷitāṉa vaḻiyetuveṉṟu kāṇpatē eṉakku vaḻakkam. ilēcilē ceytu muṭikkakkūṭāta kāriyamāyiruntāl māttiram ciṟitu cuṟṟāṉa vaḻiyiṟ cella muyalvēṉ. iṉivirittuc collavēṇṭuvatillai; pōtum! ippōtu mālaikku uṇavu cittamāyirukkum nām ataṉai uṇpēm. uṇṭapiṉ nī eṉpakkattiṟ paṭuttu ayarvu tīrttukkoṇṭu viṭiyaṟkālaiyil nīlakiri nakarattil uṉ talaivaṉiṭam pōyccēr eṉṟu moḻintāṉ.

atikāram - 2
vaḻi maṟittal

ippōtu collappaṭum viṣayaṅkaḷ muṉṉatikārattiṟ kūṟiya karumaṅkaḷ naṭanta mūṉṟunāṉku nāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ nērntaṉavākum.
ñāyiṟu kīḻtticaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟukiṉṟa camayattil āṟupēr kuṭakiluruntu nīlakiri nakarattiṟkuc cellum pātaiyai aṭuttu aṭarntirukkuṅ kuṟuṅkāṭṭiṉ nīḻalil taṅki yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ ēṟi vanta kutiraikaḷiṉ nilaimaiyaip pārkkum pōtu avarkaḷ iraviṟ cila nāḻikaikaḷāka vaḻi naṭantu vantirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. ivarkaḷ taṅkiya iṭam nīlakiri nakarattiṟku nāṟpatumail tūrattiṟku ippāl, mēṟkaṇavāy malaittoṭarukku arukāmaiyil uḷḷatu. oruyāṇṭil mukkāṟ paṅkuparuvam mika iṉiyatāy viḷaṅku vatāṉa ammalaināṭṭil, ivviṭiyaṟ kālamāṉatu mikavum aḻakitākap polivuṟṟatu. paṟavaikkuḻāṅkaḷ maraṅkaḷiṉmēṟ pāṭik koṇṭiruntaṉa; aṉṉappuṭkaḷ kālvāykaḷiṟ cerukkuṭaṉē mitantu koṇṭiruntaṉa; koḻuviya malarkaḷ itaḻvirintu kāṟṟu vāṭṭattil naṟu maṇam parappiṉa; aḷaviṟantaṉavāy muḻumuḻup parumaṉuṭaiya vāṉa navamaṇi kaḷuṅ kulaikulaiyāyt taḻaikaḷiṉ iṭaiyē toṅkik koṇ ṭiruppatupōlat tōṉṟum. mātuḷam paḻam, elumiccampaḻam, koṭimuntirippaḻam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṟattiṇintu viḷaṅkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ mēl ñāyiṟṟiṉ iḷaṅkatirkaḷ tōyntu miṉumiṉuveṉṟu miḷirntaṉa; vāṉameṉṉum nīlavitāṉattiṉ mēl mēkakkaṟai oruciṟitum illāmaiyāl atuteḷintu kāṇappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. māṟṟuyarnta poṟ kōḷampōṟ pirakācikkum iḷañāyiṟu taṉ katirkaḷaic cūḻavirittu nīlaniṟattōṭu viracum iṭantavira, avvāṉiṉ teḷinīlavaṇṇam iṭai yaṟuntu pōkāmal orēoḻuṅkāyt toṭarpuṟṟu vaḷaivāyt tikaḻntatu.
ippōtu ikkuṟuṅ kāṭṭilirunta āṭkaḷ aṟuvarum, muṉṉatikārattil nāṅkāṭṭiya kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṟ cērnta varkaḷ eṉṟaṟiya vēṇṭum. nām muṉcolliyavāṟē uṭai uṭuttup paṭaikkalaṅkaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar. āyiṉum, ippōtu cuḻal tuppākkikaḷait tām vēṇṭiyapōtu kaiyāḷat takkavakaiyāy mutukiṉ mēṟṟoṅka viṭṭiruppatu oṉṟu tāṉ vēṟupāṭākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷukkut talaivaṉpōṟ kāṇappaṭṭa oruvaṉ ivviṭantāṉ. nām terintu koṇṭapaṭi nām virumpiya kāriyam ippōtu ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkum nāḻikaikkuḷ muṭintuviṭum. vāruṅkaḷ. nammuṭaiya kutiraikaḷai ikkāṭṭuk kuḷḷē koṇṭupōy viṭṭu mikunta nēramuṅ koḻumpul mēyac ceyvippōm. eṉṟu aḻaittāṉ.
iruḷā, ippōtu nī colliya kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ? eṉṟu maṟṟoruvaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.
avai curukkamāyiruntālum, nammuṭaiya talaivaṉ eppōtum namakku iṭuṅkaṭṭaḷaikaḷaip pōlavē kaṭumaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu iruḷaṉ viṭai pakarntaṉaṉ.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, avai mēyappaṭuvataṟkum iṭañ ceykiṉṟaṉa. nammuṭaiya talaivaṉākiya apperiya payaṅkara māṉa nallāṉ taṉ karuttaiyum tāṉ muṭikkuṅ kāriyaṅ kaḷaiyum eṉakkuṅkūṭac colluvatillai. iṅkē nammai yaṉuppi viṭṭatupōlavē tāṉum maṟṟōr iṭattiṟkup pōyiruppatai ūkikkumpōtu, cantiraṉāṟ collappaṭṭa oru ceyti allatu ōr upāyattiṟkum itaṟkum etō campantam iruppatākat terikiṉṟatu eṉṟu iruḷaṉ coṉṉāṉ.
cilakālamāy mūṉṟu nāṉku taram nammuṭaiya talaivaṉiṭam vantu koṇṭirukkum cantiraṉ eṉkiṉṟa avviḷaiñaṉ yār eṉṟu avarkaḷil maṟṟoruvaṉ 
viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku iruḷaṉ nīlakirip paṭṭiṉattilirukkum paṇak kāraṉ oruvaṉ vīṭṭiṟku avaṉ uriyavaṉ eṉpataṟkumēl avaṉaippaṟṟi eṉakku oṉṟumē teriyātu. itu niṟka. nām iṅṅaṉam vīṇpēccup pēcikkoṇṭu kālattaik kaḻippatai viṭa, nallāṉ eṉṉiṭattuc colliya ēṟpāṭukaḷai uṅkaḷukkuc collukiṉṟēṉ kēḷuṅkaḷ! nām āṟupēr irukkiṉṟōm; nām etirkkavēṇṭiyatu mūṉṟupēraittāṉ eṉpatu teriyum. inta mūvaril oruvaṉ atikārattāṉum poruḷāṉum uyarnta pautta vālipaṉ eṉṟum, maṟṟai iruvarum avaṉukkuc cēvakar eṉṟum aṟivīrkaḷāka. avarkaḷ cevvaiyāṉa kutiraikaḷiṉmēl āyutaṅkaḷait tarittu varuvārkaḷ eṉpatil cantēkam illai; ataṉmēlum avarkaḷuṭaiya maṉō tairiyam vēṟēyāka irukkiṉṟatu. kūṭumāyiṉ avarkaḷmēl irattañ coṭṭāmal mūvaraiyuñ ciṟaiyākap piṭikkavēṇṭuvatu nammuṭaiya kaṭamai eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
ā! nām tuppākkiḷai upayōkikka nērumāyiṉ aṅṅaṉam irattañ cintāmaṟ piṭippatu mikavum varutta māyirukkumē eṉṟu avarkaḷil maṟṟoruvaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku iruḷaṉ itaṟku māṟākattāṉ uṅkaḷukku itu collap pōkiṉṟēṉ. ammūvaraiyum nammuṭaiya malaik kōṭṭaikku uyiruṭaṉ koṇṭu pōyviṭṭāl, nām eṭutta kāriyamum nan talaivaṉ iṭṭa kaṭṭaḷaiyum mikavuñ cemmaiyāka muṭintaṉavākum. ātalāl, uṅkaḷāl āṉa maṭṭum pārttuk kūṭāta viṭattu māttiram uṅkaḷaik kāttukkoḷḷum poruṭṭut tuppākkikaḷai upayōkap paṭuttuṅkaḷ. itutāṉ nallāṉ iṭṭa kaṭṭaḷaiyum eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
mikavum valimaiyuṭaiya malaināṭarkaḷāṉa nām aṟuvarum ōr imaippoḻutil antap pauttarkaḷ mūvaraiyum piṭiyāviṭṭāl, appuṟam eṉṉa irukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟu maṟṟoruvaṉ coṉṉāṉ.
ataṉmēl vaḻimaṟikkappaṭum pirayāṇikaḷ varukiṉṟa pātaiyai iruḷaṉ paricōtittukkoṇṭu celvāṉā 
yiṉāṉ; aṅṅaṉam pōkaiyil, pātaiyiṉ .irupakkaṅ kaḷiṉumuḷḷa kuṟuṅkāṭu oṉṟu cērntu mēlē kavintirukkum ōr iṭattait terintukoṇṭu aṅkiruntu nammuṭaiya kāriyattai niṟaivēṟṟa virumpi, ataṟku arukāmaiyil uḷḷa ōriṭattiṟ kutiraikaḷai naṭattikkoṇṭupōyp pulmēya viṭuttu, ovvoruvarum oru noṭiyil ēṟi etirppataṟku vēṇṭum oḻuṅkukaḷellām ceytu vaittut tāṉum cittamāy patuṅkiyiruntāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam orumaṇinēraṅ kaḻintatu; kaḻintavuṭaṉ neṭun tūrattil mūṉṟu pirayāṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tuvark kāviyūṭṭiya talaiccīrā aṇin tiruntamai yāṉum, kuṟippiṭṭa kālattil mūṉṟu pērāy nīlakirip paṭṭiṉattiṟkuc cellumpātaiyil vantu koṇṭiruntamaiyāṉum, tām etirpārtta pirayāṇikaḷ ivarkaḷ tāmeṉṟu akkaḷvar uṟuticeytaṉar. kutiraimēṟ cavāriceytuvarum mūvaril oruvaṉ maṟṟiruvarkkuc ciṟitu muṉṉēṟi vantaṉaṉ; ivaṉ taṉ mārpil iṟuka muṭintu aṇintirukkuṅ kuppāyattiṉ poṟcarikaimēlum, poṉ miṉukkuc ceyta vāḷuṟai mēlum, kutiraiyiṉ uyarnta cēṇaṅkaliṉam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mēlum ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirkaḷ tōyntu oḷirntaṉa. ākaiyāṟ kuṭakiliruntu nīlakiri nakarukkup pōkum celvattāṉum, atikārattāṉum uyarnta pautta iḷaiñaṉ ivaṉtāṉ eṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamuṟa iṭamillai. iṅṅaṉam ētum camucayamiṉṟi muṉṉēvantu koṇṭirukku miḷaiñaṉ kaṭṭiḷa maiyōṉāy mikavum aḻakuṭaiyaṉāy iruppatum, avaṉ piṉṉē varum maṉitar iruvarum iḷamaikaḻintu tiṇṇiya uṭampuṭaiyō rāyiruttalum aṅkup patuṅkiyirunta kaḷvar kaṇṭukoṇṭaṉar.
uṭaṉē iruḷaṉ oru kuṟi ceytaluṅ kaḷvarellārum maraṅkaḷiṉ naṭuviṟ kutiraimēl ēṟikkoṇṭu, muntirik koṭikaḷ piṉṉal piṉṉalāy neruṅkit tirai maṟaippup pōṟ paṭarntirukkuṅ kuṟuṅkāṭṭōrattil, pirayāṇikaḷ kaṇkaḷukkut teṉpaṭāmal etirppataṟkuc cittamāy vantu niṉṟaṉar. vantuniṟṟalum, iraṇṭām taramum orukuṟi ceyyap paṭṭatu. uṭaṉē māṉ mantaiyāyiṉuṅ kāṭṭu vilaṅkiṉaṅ kaḷāyiṉuñ caṭutiyil taḻaikaḷāṉum paḻaṅkaḷāṉum maṟaikkap paṭṭa tiraiyaik kīṟik kiḻittup pukuntatupōla mēl vantu viḻuntu, iruvar nīlalōcaṉaṉaiyum maṟṟai nālvar avaṉpiṉ vanta maṟṟiruvaraiyum vaḷaittuk koṇṭaṉar.
ivvāṟu akkaḷvar evvaḷavu curukkeṉap pukunta ṉarō avvaḷavu viraivil appauttar mūvar valatukai yiṉum vāḷ uṟai kaḻikkappaṭṭu miṉṉiṉa; atē camayattiṟ cēṇattiṟ cerukiyirunta kait tuppākkiyiṉai iṭatu kaiyāl ovvoru varum uruvi yiḻuttaṉar. ākavē akkaḷvar etirpārtta vaṇṇam avarkaḷ kāriyam niṟaivēṟuvatu aritāykkāṇap paṭṭatu; iruḷaṉum maṟṟoru kaḷvaṉum nīlalōcaṉaṉ mēṟ kutittu viḻavē, avaṉ orukaiyāṟ cuḻaṟṟi vīciya vāḷ oruvaṉ mēṟ paṭumpoḻutu, avaṉ maṟṟaik kaiyiṟ piṭitta tuppākki maṟṟoruvaṉaic cuṭṭu kīḻē vīḻttiyatu. iruḷaṉō avvāḷvīccukkut tappikkoṇṭu oru kaiyil vāḷēnti maṟṟoru kaiyāl avaṉaip piṭikka muyaṉṟāṉ. nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉ pakaivaṉaippōlavē taṉ kutiraiyait tiṟampaṭa naṭattuñ cāturiyam naṉku vāyntava ṉātalāl kutiraiyiṉ kaṭivāḷattaip piṉṉukku piṭittu iḻuttut taṉ pakaivaṉ taṉṉaik kāttukkoḷḷutaṟku iṭañceytāṉ. ivvāṟu cilanēram iruvarkkum pōr mūṇṭu naṭakkaiyil, tiṭīreṉṟu iruḷaṉ taṉ kutirai mēṉiṉṟum vaḻuvi maṟaintu pōyiṉāṉ; appaṭi maṟaikaiyil nīlalōcaṉaṉ tāṉ avaṉmēl eṟinta paṭaivīccu avaṉai kollamāṭṭāmaiyāl avaṉ maṟaintu pōṉatu ētō ōr upāyamāka irukkiṉṟateṉa uṭaṉē terintu koṇṭāṉ. teriyavē avaṉ taṉ kutiraiyiṉiṉṟum pāyntu pakaivaṉaip piṭittuk koḷḷa muyalāmal iccaṇṭaiyiṉ muṭipai yaṟiyumporuṭṭu oru kaṇanēram vāḷāviruntaṉaṉ.
uṇmaiyākavē iruḷaṉ avvāṟu maṟaintupōṉatu oru tantiram. malaināṭṭukkaḷvar pōrmukattil ittakaiya tantiraṅkaḷ ceytu veṟṟiyaṭaivatil mikavun tiṟamaiyu ṭaiyavarkaḷāy iruntaṉar; pōr naṭantukoṇṭirukkaiyil caṭutiyilē kutirai mutukiṉ mēliruntu vaḻuvi ataṉ vayiṟṟiṉ kīḻppukuntu kāl utaiyum vaḷaiyattiṟ kālviral māṭṭi iraṇṭu kaikaḷāṉum piṭariyaip piṭitta vaṇṇamāy toṟṟik koṇṭiruppar. ïªj¢ Nœ¢á Æida¿ahj vâÇ j‹ FâiuÆÅ‹W« ghŒªJ ï¡Fâiu nkš VWth dhÆD«., aṉṟi etiriyōṭip pōyiṉāṉ eṉṟu niṉaintu taṉ kutiraiyai viṭṭuk kīḻ iṟaṅkuvāṉā yiṉum uṭaṉē avaṉ mēṟ pulipōṟ pāyntu paṟṟik koḷḷuvar. itē vakaiyāy nīlalōcaṉaṉait taṉ kutiraiyiṉiṉṟum iṟakkum poruṭṭu iruḷaṉ ivvāṟu ōr upāyañ ceytāṉ.
ōr imaikoṭṭil nīlalōcaṉaṉ tāṉ aiyappaṭṭatu uṇmaiyeṉṟu kaṇṭu kutirai cēṇattiniṉṟum uruviṉa taṉkait tuppākkiyai iruḷaṉ maṇṭaikku nērāyk kuṟikāṭṭit taṉakkuk kīḻppaṭiyumpaṭi kēṭṭaṉaṉ. itaṟ kiṭaiyiṟ pōrpurintu koṇṭiruppōrkkup piṉṉēyiruntu kuṇṭukaḷ vantu viḻuntaṉa; iccamayattiṟ kaḷvar iruvar muṉṉē kāṟṟiṉuṅ kaṭuki vantu iruḷaṉai nōkki uṉṉaik kāppāṟṟik koḷ eṉṟaṉar.
miṉṉal tōṉṟuvatupōla iruḷaṉ kutirai appuṟan tuḷḷippāyntu pōyiṟṟu; atē camayattil nīlalōcaṉaṉ tuppākkiyiṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭṭa kuṇṭukaḷ muṟipaṭṭup paṟantōṭuṅkaḷvaṉ kātōramāyk kiṟukiṟu veṉṟu cuḻaṉṟu pōyiṉa. paṟantōṭiṉa iruḷaṉ nūṟṟaimpatu muḻantūram pōṉavuṭaṉē taṉ kutiraiyai niṟutti ippuṟan tiruppi mutukiṟ ṟoṅkaviṭṭirunta kaittuppāk kiyiṉai aviḻkkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. nīlalōcaṉaṉ kūriya kaṇkaḷukku itu terintavuṭaṉē, malaināṭṭukkaḷvar kuṟi piḻaiyāmaṟ cuṭuvatil vallavarkaḷeṉpataṉai uṇarntu, tāṉ ataṟku ilakkāy akappaṭāmal, taṉ kutiraiyai muṭukkik kuṟuṅkāṭṭiṉuḷḷē pukuntuppōyt tappiṉāṉ. ivaṉ itaṟkuḷ nuḻainta piṟkaṇattē tuppākkik kuṇṭukaḷ kiṟukiṟuveṉṟu cuḻaṉṟu ceṉṟaṉa. iruḷaṉ taṉ etiri tappippōyviṭṭatai aṟintu kutiraiyait tiruppikkoṇṭu viraintu pōyiṉāṉ.
itaṟku muṉṉamē nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉpiṉvanta kāvalāḷar eṅṅaṉamāyiṉār eṉpataṉai oru noṭip poḻutilē aṟintukoṇṭaṉaṉ; ippōtu ataṉaic cevvaiyāka aḷantaṟivataṟkuc camayam vāyttatu. kaḷvar aṟuvaril iruvar appautta iḷaiñaṉai maṟittaṉar. ivviruvaril oruvaṉ cuṭappaṭṭu viḻuntiṟantāṉ; maṟṟai yōṉ piḻaittōṭip pōyiṉāṉ. kāvalāḷar iruvaraiyum vaḷaittukkoṇṭa kaḷvar nālvaril, iruvar talaiyiṟ cuṭappaṭṭu uyir oḻintu nilattiṟ kiṭantaṉar. maṟṟiruvaril, oruvaṉ kattiveṭṭiṉāl vāḷēntiyatōḷ aṟupaṭṭum, maṟṟoruvaṉ kaḻuttu muḷḷelumpu oṭintum etirniṟka lāṟṟātu muṉṉē coṉṉavāṟāy ōṭippōyiṉar. ākaiyāl nīlalōcaṉaṉ kāvalāḷarum tan talaivaṉaip pōlavē veṟṟimaṟañ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum ivar kaḷil oruvaṉukku māttiram iṭatu tōṭpuṟattuc cataiyil vāḷ aḻuntu veṭṭukkāyamiruntatu; itupaṟṟi avar ciṟitum maṉantaḷartaliṉṟi tammuṭaiya talaivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻntaṉar.
kollappaṭṭa kaḷvar mūvaruṭaiya kutiraikaḷum ivarkaḷ kaṭ pārvaikku akappaṭāmal avviṭattai akaṉṟu ōṭippōy viṭṭaṉa. nīlalōcaṉaṉum avaṉ kāvalāḷarum vaḻi naṭuviṟ kiṭanta appiṇaṅkaḷai akaṟṟik koṭimuntirip pantar niḻaṟkīḻ kiṭatti viṭṭut tām pōm vaḻiyiṟ celva rāyiṉar. cellum pōtu iṅṅaṉam vantu vaḻimaṟittavarkaḷ vēṟār nōkkamumiṉṟik koḷḷaiyiṭutalē kuṟippāka vuṭaiya āṟalaik kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṟ cērntavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇiṉar.
appōtu nīlalōcaṉaṉ avaraippārttu akkoṭiya varkaḷ kuṟuṅkāṭṭil maṟaintiruntapaṭiyē nammaiccuṭāmal etirēṟivantu pōriṭṭatu eṉakku mikavum putumaiyāy irukkiṉṟatu. appaṭi avarkaḷ cuṭṭiruntāl nām ellām uyiriḻantiruppōm. malaināṭṭuk kaḷvar kuṟitavaṟāmaṟ cuṭuvatil mikavum vallavarāyiṟṟē eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
ataikkēṭṭatum, aṭarntu paḻuttuppōṉa tāṭiyum, maṉavuṟuti, ūkkam, poṟai mutaliya uyarkuṇaṅkaḷ naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa mukamum, taṭittut tiṇinta uṭampum uṭaiya vaṉāṉa mūttakāvalāḷaṉ eṅkaḷ araciṉ celvamē, nammiṭattuk koḷḷai koḷvatuṭaṉ nammaic ciṟaiyākap piṭittuk koṇṭupōyt tam malai araṇil vaittiruntu viṭutalai peṟumporuṭṭup peruntokaiyāṉa poruḷum aṭaiyalām eṉkiṉṟa nōkkattōṭu avarkaḷ nammai etirttavarkaḷāka eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟaṉaṉ.
ataṟku nīlalōcaṉaṉ eṉ nampakamuḷḷa kēcarivīra nī ūkittuc colliyatu eṉakkup poruttamākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. am maṟavarkaruttu atuvāyiṉ. avar tōlvi yaṭaintatum nām veṟṟiyaṭaintatum avarkaḷuṭaiya nalla ēṟpāṭṭiṉāl allavā? avarkaḷ mutaṟkaṇattilēyē nammaic cuṭṭuk kollāmaṟ kiṭṭa vantu etirttu nīlakiri nāṭṭārkkuriya valimaiyiṉaiyum kuṭakunāṭṭārkkuriya valimaiyiṉaiyum paricōtittu aṟintu koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
atu kēṭṭa iḷaiyakāvalāḷaṉāṉa viyākkiravīraṉ eṅkaḷ aracurimaic celvamē, mutaṉmutaṟ piṉmutuku kāṭṭiyōṭiṉa paḻikārar iruvarun taṅkaḷāl oṉṟuñ cāyātu eṉṟu kaṇṭavuṭaṉē tuppākkiyai eṭuttuc cuṭṭaṉar; eṉ coṟpaṭiyellām vaṭṭamāyttirintum muṉṉōṭiyum piṉṉōṭiyum naṭakkiṉṟa eṉ kutiraiyiṉ ilākavatti ṉālaṉṟō taṅkaḷai vicuvacikkum ūḻiyakkāra ṉāṉa aṭiyēṉ tappip piḻaittut taṅkaḷōṭu ippōtu pēcik koṇṭirukkiṉṟēṉ! eṉṟaṉaṉ.
eṉṟatuṅ kēcari vīraṉ eṉ kātarukilum vicaikkāṟṟu viṟuviṟuveṉṟu vīcutal pōlak kuṇṭukaḷ cuḻaṉṟu pōyiṉa. atukiṭakka nām kautamacākkiyarai vaṇaṅkuvēmāka! veṟṟi nampakkamāyiṟṟu. viyākkira vīraṉ tōḷil vāḷ veṭṭup paṭṭatu oṉṟu tavira, nām vēṟōr iṭaiyūṟu miṉṟi vantu cērntamai viyappēyām. eṉṟu moḻintāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam pēcikkoṇṭē nīlalōcaṉaṉum avaṉ kāvalāḷarum vaḻi piṭittup pōyiṉar; araciḷaiñaṉum ippōtu taṉ kāvalāḷaruṭaṉ cērntu pōyiṉāṉ; maṟṟoru muṟaiyum vaḻimaṟittal nēriṭuvatāyiṉ ataṟkuc cittamāy upayōkittaṟporuṭṭu mūvarun tuppākkiyaik kaiyil ēntiya vaṇṇamāyc ceṉṟaṉar.
cellumpōtu ciṟitu nēraṅkaḻittuk kēcarivīraṉ eṅkaḷ aracē nammai vaḻimaṟittavarkaḷ, cōḻa nāṭ
ṭārkkum malaināṭṭārkkum cilakālamāy periyatōr accattiṉai yuṇṭupaṇṇivaruṅ kaḷvar talaivaṉaic cērnta varkaḷāyirukkalām eṉpatu taṅkaḷukkut tōṉṟavillaiyā? eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ.
ataṟku nīlalōcaṉaṉ nallāṉaik kuṟippikkiṉṟa ṉaiyō? ām, avaṉaippaṟṟip putupputuk kataikaḷ innīlakiri malaiccāral ellaiyait tāṇṭik kuṭakilirukkum nammuṭaiya cevikaḷukkum eṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; uṉakku uṇmaiyaic collukiṉṟēṉ, nāṉ antak kataikaḷai oruciṟitum nampuvatēyillai. nallāṉ eṉṉum ōr āḷē uṇṭeṉpatu peruṅkaṭṭu, avaṉ oru maṉappēy tāṉ eṉṟaṉaṉ.
eṅkaḷ talaiva, nallāṉ eṉpatu kaṭṭāyirukkalām, meykāppāṉa cila turuppukkaḷuṭaṉ nīṅkaḷ pattiramāyp pōkalāmeṉṟu cilar kūṟiya uṟutimoḻikkum māṟākac ceṉṟa iraṇṭoru nāḻikai yaṉupavattiṉālēyē ippakkaṅkaḷil āyutapāṇikaḷāṉa āṟalaikaḷvar kūṭṭam uṇṭeṉpatu namakkup pulappaṭavillaiyā? eṉṟu kēcari vīraṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku nīlalōcaṉaṉ  ippirāyaṇañ ceyyumpaṭi eṉṉait tūṇṭutal ceytavōr tirumukaṅ koṇṭu vantavaṉāṉa anta nīlakiri nakarattiḷaiñaṉṟāṉ aṅṅaṉam uṟutiyurai moḻintāṉ. avaṉ tāṉ meyyeṉṟu nampiṉataiyē kūṟiṉāṉ, atuvēyumaṉṟi nām pōvataip piṟar kavaṉiyāta vaṇṇamāy aṉṟō pirāyaṇañ ceyyumpaṭi kaṟpikkappaṭṭōm--ā! atō etirē oru kuṭicai tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu! aṅkē nām kaṭukac ceṉṟu, aṅkuḷḷōrkku iṅkē nikaḻntaṉayāvum terivippōm. avarkaḷ nām vaḻiyilē pōṭṭu vanta piṇaṅkaḷai appuṟap paṭuttuvataṟku vēṇṭuvatu ētēṉuñceyvar.
appaṭiyē ammūvaruṅ kuṭicaiyaṇṭaipōyc cērntaṉar; aṅkirunta mutiyōṉ oruvaṉukku nikaḻnta vellāñ collap paṭṭaṉa. nallāṉ naṭattum āṟalai toḻilkaḷ iṉitu naṭaipeṟukiṉṟa mēṟkaṇavāy malaiccāralukku neṭun tūrattēyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil iṅṅaṉam vaḻimaṟippu nērntataip paṟṟi avaṉ mikavum viyappaṭaintavaṉ pōṟ kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. ataṉōṭu mūṉṟu pauttarāl mika valiyarāṉa āṟukaḷvar muṟiyaṭikkap paṭṭataṉaippaṟṟi avaṉ piṉṉum mikunta viyappaṭaintu avarkaḷ coṟkaḷil avanampikkaippaṭavē, avarkaḷ, kollappaṭṭa ak koṭiya kaḷvar mūvar uṭampum piṇamāyp pātaiyiṟ kiṭattal kāṇalām eṉṟu uṟutiyuraittaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam malaināṭṭuk kāvalatikārikaḷukkut tāñ colla vēṇṭiya kaṭamaiyaic ceytu viṭṭuc, cilanēram ak kuṭicaiyil viṭutikoṇṭa piṉ nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉ kāvalāḷar iruvaruṭaṉ payaṇam paṇṇap pukuntaṉaṉ. ik kāvalāḷar iruvarum veṟum ūḻiyakkārar allar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ ciṟanta maṉṉaṉ ākaiyāl, avaṉuṭaṉ vanta ivarkaḷ pirapukkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiya vēṇṭum.
iṅku ivvaraciḷaiñaṉ, cēkari vīraṉ, viyākkira vīraṉ eṉṉum iruvaruṭaṉum pōyk koṇṭirukkum pōtu, namakkunalla camayam vāyttamaiyāl avaṉ uṭampiṉ aḻakiya tōṟṟattaip paṟṟi varuṇittuc colla virumpukiṉṟōm. ivaṉ mikavum aḻakāyiruntāṉ eṉṟu muṉṉamē potuvākac coṉṉōm, ivaṉukkuk kariya aḷakamuṅ kariya periya viḻikaḷum iruntaṉa; ivaṉ mīcaiyāṉatu kaival ōviyaṉ tukilikaiyāl vaṉaintavāṟu pōlap paḷapaḷaveṉṟu mēlutaṭṭiṉ mēṟ curuṇṭiruntatu.  ivaṉ mūkkuk kaḻuku pōṟ ciṟitē nuṉiyil vaḷaintiruntatu, ituvum mukattil uḷḷa maṟṟai uṟuppukaḷum mika melliyataṉmai  yuṭaiyavāyiruntaṉa. ivaṉ uṭampiṉ niṟam perumpālārkku uḷḷatu pōlak kariyatāyillai āyiṉum ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāṟ ciṟitu paḻuppu vaṇṇamāyt tōṉṟutaliṉ ivaṉuṭaiya niṟam iḷamaiyuruviṟ ciṟituparuvamutirccik kāṭṭiṟṟu. illā viṭṭāl, ivaṉ niṟam iṉṉun teḷivāy iḷamaip paruvattiṟku icaintatāy viḷaṅkum. ivvāṟu uṭampiṉ niṟam paḻuppāyt tōṉṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam āṇpālarkkuriya vēṭṭaiyāṭutalil ivaṉ mikunta viruppa muṭaiyōṉāyp pakaṟkālaṅkaḷil tirivatoṉṟē eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. vēṭṭaiyāṭuvatiluṅ kutiraiyēṟṟattilum ivaṉ nirampiya tiṟamai vāyntiruntaṉaṉ. ciṟitu nērattiṟku muṉṉē vantu vaḻimaṟitta kaḷvarkaḷai añcātu ivaṉ puṟaṅkaṇṭu veṟṟi ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉataippōlavē, iṉṉuṅ kaṭumaiyāṉa pōrmukaṅ kaḷilellām aṅṅaṉamē maṟañciṟantu tikaḻn taṉaṉ, ivaṉiṭattup poṟumaiyum tayāḷakuṇamuṅ kuṭi koṇṭi runtaṉa; iṉṉum avaṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷiṉellām āṇṭaṉmaiyōṭu kūṭiya oru peruntaṉmai ciṟantu kāṭṭuvatāyiṟṟu. accam eṉpataṉai ivaṉ aṟiyavē māṭṭāṉ. apāyam eṉkiṉṟa collaik kēṭpiṉum nakaiyāṭuvāṉ. ivviyal piṉaṉāyiṉum, oru pirāṇi varuntutalaik kāṇiṉum piṟacīvar tuṉpuṟutalaik kēṭpiṉum ivaṉ kaṇkaḷil nīr muttu muttāyt tuḷikkum
ivaṉukku ippōtu vayatu irupattoṉṟu, ivaṉuṭampu uyarnta pūṅkompupōl melliyatāyum, tiruntiya amaippuṭaiyatāyum mataṉavēḷ pōlak kaikāl mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷ nīḷamāyum tuvaṇṭaṉa. vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa veḷḷaic calavaikkallil ivaṉ uṭampiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷai naṉ mātiriyāka vaittu uḷiyāṟ cetukki iḻaittu oru patumai amaikkappaṭumāyiṉ atu vaḻuvaṟṟa āṇṭaṉmaiyiṉaik kuṟikkum vaṭivamāyp piracitti yaṭaiyumeṉpatu tiṇṇa mēyām. ākavē avaṉ aṅkaṅkaḷiṉ amaippiṉ aḻakai yām eṅṅaṉam varuṇikka māṭṭuvēm! avaṉ neṟṟi akaṉṟu amaintu kiṭantatu; taṭippiṉṟi mika murintu vaḷainta ivaṉuṭaiya puruvaṅkaḷ mikavum kariyavāy iruntaṉa; kaṇṇiṟaippai cevvaiyākat tiṟantiruntaṉa; viḻikaḷ parumaṉāy imaikaḷiṟ peṇkaḷuk kiruppatupōṟ karukiyaṭarnta mayirvaricaiyum iruntaṉa; taṉṉai uyarttip piṟarait tāḻttutal, cerukkaṭaital, taṟukaṇmai mutalāṉa tīyakuṇaṅkaḷ ivaṉiṭattu oruciṟitum illai. āyiṉum āṇṭaṉmaiyum pōrmukattu añcāmaiyum eṉkiṉṟa uyarkuṇaṅkaḷ vāykkappeṟṟa iḷaiñarkkut tamuyarvu tāmēyaṟiyum ōr uṇarcci iyaṟkaiyāka uṇṭātalpōla ivaṉiṭattum ippeṟṟip paṭṭatōr uṇarcci tōṉṟutal uṇṭu. ivaṉ kaṉṉamīcai vaittirāviṭiṉum,  mēl itaḻil uḷḷa kariyamīcaiyum, elumiccampaḻattaiviṭac ciṟitu paḻuppākat tōṉṟum mēṉiyiṉ niṟamum, etaṟkum añcāta kaṇkaḷ nōkkamum ivaṉ mukattil ōr āṇṭaṉmai viḷaṅkat tōṟṟuvittaṉa.
